Input

  "filePath": "/source/standards/karpathy-guidelines.mdc"

Output

<path>/source/standards/karpathy-guidelines.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code to avoid overcomplication, make surgical changes, surface assumptions, and define verifiable success criteria.
3: alwaysApply: true
4: ---
5: 
6: # Karpathy behavioral guidelines
7: 
8: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Merge with project-specific instructions as needed.
9: 
10: **Tradeoff:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks, use judgment.
11: 
12: ## 1. Think Before Coding
13: 
14: **Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**
15: 
16: Before implementing:
17: 
18: - State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.
19: - If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.
20: - If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.
21: - If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.
22: 
23: ## 2. Simplicity First
24: 
25: **Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.**
26: 
27: - No features beyond what was asked.
28: - No abstractions for single-use code.
29: - No "flexibility" or "configurability" that wasn't requested.
30: - No error handling for impossible scenarios.
31: - If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.
32: 
33: Ask yourself: "Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?" If yes, simplify.
34: 
35: ## 3. Surgical Changes
36: 
37: **Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.**
38: 
39: When editing existing code:
40: 
41: - Don't "improve" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.
42: - Don't refactor things that aren't broken.
43: - Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.
44: - If you notice unrelated dead code, mention it - don't delete it.
45: 
46: When your changes create orphans:
47: 
48: - Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.
49: - Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless asked.
50: 
51: The test: Every changed line should trace directly to the user's request.
52: 
53: ## 4. Goal-Driven Execution
54: 
55: **Define success criteria. Loop until verified.**
56: 
57: Transform tasks into verifiable goals:
58: 
59: - "Add validation" → "Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass"
60: - "Fix the bug" → "Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass"
61: - "Refactor X" → "Ensure tests pass before and after"
62: 
63: For multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:
64: 
65: ```
66: 1. [Step] → verify: [check]
67: 2. [Step] → verify: [check]
68: 3. [Step] → verify: [check]
69: ```
70: 
71: Strong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria ("make it work") require constant clarification.
72: 
73: ---
74: 
75: **These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes.

(End of file - total 75 lines)
</content>

read · completed

Input

{
  "filePath": "/source/standards/ponytail.mdc"
}

Output

<path>/source/standards/ponytail.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode. Always pick the simplest solution that works.
3: globs:
4: alwaysApply: true
5: ---
6: 
7: # Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode
8: 
9: You are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. The best code is the code never written.
10: 
11: Before writing any code, stop at the first rung that holds:
12: 
13: 1. Does this need to be built at all? (YAGNI)
14: 2. Does it already exist in this codebase? Reuse the helper, util, or pattern that's already here, don't re-write it.
15: 3. Does the standard library already do this? Use it.
16: 4. Does a native platform feature cover it? Use it.
17: 5. Does an already-installed dependency solve it? Use it.
18: 6. Can this be one line? Make it one line.
19: 7. Only then: write the minimum code that works.
20: 
21: The ladder runs after you understand the problem, not instead of it: read the task and the code it touches, trace the real flow end to end, then climb.
22: 
23: Bug fix = root cause, not symptom: a report names a symptom. Grep every caller of the function you touch and fix the shared function once — one guard there is a smaller diff than one per caller, and patching only the path the ticket names leaves a sibling caller still broken.
24: 
25: Rules:
26: 
27: - No abstractions that weren't explicitly requested.
28: - No new dependency if it can be avoided.
29: - No boilerplate nobody asked for.
30: - Deletion over addition. Boring over clever. Fewest files possible.
31: - Shortest working diff wins, but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.
32: - Question complex requests: "Do you actually need X, or does Y cover it?"
33: - Pick the edge-case-correct option when two stdlib approaches are the same size, lazy means less code, not the flimsier algorithm.
34: - Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path.
35: 
36: Not lazy about: understanding the problem (read it fully and trace the real flow before picking a rung, a small diff you don't understand is just laziness dressed up as efficiency), input validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs (the platform is never the spec ideal, a clock drifts, a sensor reads off), anything explicitly requested. Lazy code without its check is unfinished: non-trivial logic leaves ONE runnable check behind, the smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks (an assert-based demo/self-check or one small test file; no frameworks, no fixtures). Trivial one-liners need no test.

(End of file - total 36 lines)
</content>

Always pick the simplest solution that works.\nglobs:\nalwaysApply: true\n---\n\n# Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode\n\nYou are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. The best code is the code never written.\n\nBefore writing any code, stop at the first rung that holds:\n\n1. Does this need to be built at all? (YAGNI)\n2. Does it already exist in this codebase? Reuse the helper, util, or pattern that's already here, don't re-write it.\n3. Does the standard library already do this? Use it.\n4. Does a native platform feature cover it? Use it.\n5. Does an already-installed dependency solve it? Use it.\n6. Can this be one line? Make it one line.\n7. Only then: write the minimum code that works.\n\nThe ladder runs after you understand the problem, not instead of it: read the task and the code it touches, trace the real flow end to end, then climb.\n\nBug fix = root cause, not symptom: a report names a symptom. Grep every caller of the function you touch and fix the shared function once — one guard there is a smaller diff than one per caller, and patching only the path the ticket names leaves a sibling caller still broken.\n\nRules:\n\n- No abstractions that weren't explicitly requested.\n- No new dependency if it can be avoided.\n- No boilerplate nobody asked for.\n- Deletion over addition. Boring over clever. Fewest files possible.\n- Shortest working diff wins, but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.\n- Question complex requests: \"Do you actually need X, or does Y cover it?\"\n- Pick the edge-case-correct option when two stdlib approaches are the same size, lazy means less code, not the flimsier algorithm.\n- Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path.\n\nNot lazy about: understanding the problem (read it fully and trace the real flow before picking a rung, a small diff you don't understand is just laziness dressed up as efficiency), input validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs (the platform is never the spec ideal, a clock drifts, a sensor reads off), anything explicitly requested. Lazy code without its check is unfinished: non-trivial logic leaves ONE runnable check behind, the smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks (an assert-based demo/self-check or one small test file; no frameworks, no fixtures). Trivial one-liners need no test.",

95: 		return
96: 	}

104:

228: 			}
229: 		}
230: 	}
231: 
232: 	if container.ShortID == "" {
233: 		container.ShortID = clabutils.ShortID(container.ID)
234: 	}
235: 
236: 	if runtime != nil {
237: 		container.SetRuntime(runtime)
238: 	}
239: 
240: 	return container
241: }
242: 
243: type netlinkWatcher struct {
244: 	container       *clabruntime.GenericContainer
245: 	cancel          context.CancelFunc
246: 	done            chan struct{}
247: 	includeSnapshot bool
248: 	includeStats    bool
249: 	statsInterval   time.Duration
250: 	nsHandle        netns.NsHandle
251: 	// reported holds what each link was last reported as, by its index in the
252: 	// container's namespace.
253: 	reported map[int]reportedInterface
254: }
255: 
256: // reportedInterface is what a link was last reported as.
257: type reportedInterface struct {
258: 	attributes map[string]string
259: 	// since is when the link was first reported.
260: 	since time.Time
261: }
262: 
263: // stitchMarkWindow is how long after a link is first reported its host-side
264: // stitch interface is looked for while the link itself does not change.
265: // Deploying a veth-stitch link marks that interface right after it creates the
266: // link, without a change in the node's namespace.
267: var stitchMarkWindow = time.Minute
268: 
269: // Retry settings for a watcher that could not subscribe to link updates.
270: var (
271: 	watchMaxAttempts   = 5
272: 	watchRetryDelay    = time.Second
273: 	watchMaxRetryDelay = 10 * time.Second
274: )
275: 
276: func (w *netlinkWatcher) run(ctx context.Context, registry *netlinkRegistry) {
277: 	defer close(w.done)
278: 	if w.container == nil {
279: 		return
280: 	}
281: 
282: 	defer registry.remove(w.container.ID, w)
283: 
284: 	containerName := firstContainerName(w.container)
285: 	if w.container.Runtime == nil {
286: 		log.Debugf("container %s has no runtime, skipping netlink watcher", containerName)
287: 
288: 		return
289: 	}
290: 
291: 	// The states outlive a subscription, so a resubscribe only reports what
292: 	// changed while no subscription was active.
293: 	states := make(map[int]ifaceSnapshot)
294: 
295: 	var statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample
296: 	if w.includeStats {
297: 		statsSamples = make(map[int]ifaceStatsSample)
298: 	}
299: 
300: 	initial := true
301: 	failures := 0
302: 	delay := watchRetryDelay
303: 
304: 	for {
305: 		subscribed, err := w.watch(ctx, registry, states, statsSamples, initial)
306: 		if ctx.Err() != nil {
307: 			return
308: 		}
309: 
310: 		if subscribed {
311: 			initial = false
312: 			failures = 0
313: 			delay = watchRetryDelay
314: 
315: 			log.Warnf(
316: 				"netlink subscription for container %s ended, resubscribing: %v",
317: 				containerName,
318: 				err,
319: 			)
320: 		} else {
321: 			failures++
322: 			if failures >= watchMaxAttempts {
323: 				log.Warnf("stopped watching interfaces of container %s: %v", containerName, err)
324: 
325: 				return
326: 			}
327: 
328: 			log.Debugf(
329: 				"failed to watch interfaces of container %s, retrying in %s: %v",
330: 				containerName,
331: 				delay,
332: 				err,
333: 			)
334: 		}
335: 
336: 		select {
337: 		case <-time.After(delay):
338: 		case <-ctx.Done():
339: 			return
340: 		}
341: 
342: 		delay = min(delay*2, watchMaxRetryDelay)
343: 	}
344: }
345: 
346: // watch subscribes to link updates in the container's namespace and handles
347: // them until ctx ends or the subscription fails. It reports whether it
348: // subscribed. The subscription lists the existing links itself, so no change
349: // between listing and subscribing is lost.
350: func (w *netlinkWatcher) watch(
351: 	ctx context.Context,
352: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
353: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
354: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
355: 	initial bool,
356: ) (bool, error) {
357: 	nsPath, err := waitForNamespacePath(ctx, w.container.Runtime, w.container.ID)
358: 	if err != nil {
359: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to resolve netns: %w", err)
360: 	}
361: 
362: 	nsHandle, err := netns.GetFromPath(nsPath)
363: 	if err != nil {
364: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to open netns: %w", err)
365: 	}
366: 	defer nsHandle.Close()
367: 
368: 	w.nsHandle = nsHandle
369: 
370: 	netHandle, err := netlink.NewHandleAt(nsHandle)
371: 	if err != nil {
372: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create netlink handle: %w", err)
373: 	}
374: 	defer netHandle.Close()
375: 
376: 	netemQuery := newNetemQuerier(nsHandle)
377: 	defer netemQuery.Close()
378: 
379: 	netemInfos := netemQuery.Query()
380: 
381: 	updates := make(chan netlink.LinkUpdate, 32)
382: 	done := make(chan struct{})
383: 	defer close(done)
384: 
385: 	var (
386: 		errMu     sync.Mutex
387: 		lastError error
388: 	)
389: 
390: 	opts := netlink.LinkSubscribeOptions{
391: 		Namespace:    &nsHandle,
392: 		ListExisting: true,
393: 		// An interrupted listing is reported here too. It needs no action:
394: 		// links that changed during the listing also arrive as updates.
395: 		ErrorCallback: func(err error) {
396: 			log.Debugf(
397: 				"netlink subscription for container %s: %v",
398: 				firstContainerName(w.container),
399: 				err,
400: 			)
401: 
402: 			errMu.Lock()
403: 			lastError = err
404: 			errMu.Unlock()
405: 		},
406: 	}
407: 
408: 	if err := netlink.LinkSubscribeWithOptions(updates, done, opts); err != nil {
409: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to subscribe to netlink updates: %w", err)
410: 	}
411: 
412: 	var vanished map[int]struct{}
413: 	if len(states) > 0 {
414: 		vanished = w.removeVanishedLinks(netHandle, states, statsSamples, registry)
415: 	}
416: 
417: 	// Set up netem polling interval (reuse stats interval or default to 1s).
418: 	netemPollInterval := w.statsInterval
419: 	if netemPollInterval == 0 {
420: 		netemPollInterval = time.Second
421: 	}
422: 	netemTicker := time.NewTicker(netemPollInterval)
423: 	defer netemTicker.Stop()
424: 
425: 	var (
426: 		ticker  *time.Ticker
427: 		tickerC <-chan time.Time
428: 	)
429: 	if w.includeStats && w.statsInterval > 0 {
430: 		ticker = time.NewTicker(w.statsInterval)
431: 		tickerC = ticker.C
432: 		defer ticker.Stop()
433: 	}
434: 
435: 	for {
436: 		select {
437: 		case <-tickerC:
438: 			w.collectAndEmitStats(netHandle, states, statsSamples, registry)
439: 		case <-netemTicker.C:
440: 			w.pollNetemChanges(states, registry, netemQuery)
441: 			w.reportStitchMarks(states, registry)
442: 		case <-ctx.Done():
443: 			return true, nil
444: 		case update, ok := <-updates:
445: 			if !ok {
446: 				errMu.Lock()
447: 				err := lastError
448: 				errMu.Unlock()
449: 
450: 				if err == nil {
451: 					err = errors.New("update channel closed")
452: 				}
453: 
454: 				return true, err
455: 			}
456: 
457: 			// Replies to the listing carry NLM_F_MULTI, notifications do not.
458: 			if update.Header.Flags&unix.NLM_F_MULTI != 0 {
459: 				w.processExisting(
460: 					states,
461: 					statsSamples,
462: 					update.Link,
463: 					netemInfos,
464: 					vanished,
465: 					registry,
466: 					initial,
467: 				)
468: 
469: 				continue
470: 			}
471: 
472: 			w.processUpdate(states, statsSamples, update, registry, netemQuery)
473: 		}
474: 	}
475: }
476: 
477: // processExisting records a link listed when the subscription started. On the
478: // first subscription a new link is reported as a snapshot when requested; after
479: // a resubscribe it is reported as created.
480: //
481: // The listing can be older than the check in removeVanishedLinks, so it can
482: // still hold a link that check reported as deleted. Such a link is skipped; its
483: // deletion notification follows and is ignored, as the link is not tracked.
484: func (w *netlinkWatcher) processExisting(
485: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
486: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
487: 	link netlink.Link,
488: 	netemInfos map[int]netemInfo,
489: 	vanished map[int]struct{},
490: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
491: 	initial bool,
492: ) {
493: 	if link == nil || link.Attrs() == nil {
494: 		return
495: 	}
496: 
497: 	if _, ok := vanished[link.Attrs().Index]; ok {
498: 		return
499: 	}
500: 
501: 	snapshot := withNetem(snapshotFromLink(link), netemInfos)
502: 	previous, exists := states[snapshot.Index]
503: 
504: 	if exists && snapshot.equal(previous) {
505: 		return
506: 	}
507: 
508: 	states[snapshot.Index] = snapshot
509: 	if statsSamples != nil {
510: 		if sample, ok := newStatsSample(snapshot, time.Now()); ok {
511: 			statsSamples[snapshot.Index] = sample
512: 		}
513: 	}
514: 
515: 	switch {
516: 	case exists:
517: 		w.emit(registry, "update", snapshot)
518: 	case !initial:
519: 		w.emit(registry, "create", snapshot)
520: 	case w.includeSnapshot:
521: 		w.emit(registry, "snapshot", snapshot)
522: 	}
523: }
524: 
525: // removeVanishedLinks reports the links that were deleted while no
526: // subscription was active and returns their indexes.
527: func (w *netlinkWatcher) removeVanishedLinks(
528: 	netHandle *netlink.Handle,
529: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
530: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
531: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
532: ) map[int]struct{} {
533: 	current, err := snapshotInterfaces(netHandle)
534: 	if err != nil {
535: 		log.Debugf(
536: 			"failed to list interfaces of container %s: %v",
537: 			firstContainerName(w.container),
538: 			err,
539: 		)
540: 
541: 		return nil
542: 	}
543: 
544: 	vanished := map[int]struct{}{}
545: 
546: 	for idx, snapshot := range states {
547: 		if _, ok := current[idx]; ok {
548: 			continue
549: 		}
550: 
551: 		vanished[idx] = struct{}{}
552: 
553: 		delete(states, idx)
554: 		delete(statsSamples, idx)
555: 		w.emit(registry, "delete", snapshot)
556: 	}
557: 
558: 	return vanished
559: }
560: 
561: func (w *netlinkWatcher) processUpdate(
562: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
563: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
564: 	update netlink.LinkUpdate,
565: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
566: 	netemQuery *netemQuerier,
567: ) {
568: 	if update.Link == nil {
569: 		return
570: 	}
571: 
572: 	attrs := update.Link.Attrs()
573: 	if attrs == nil {
574: 		return
575: 	}
576: 
577: 	snapshot := snapshotFromLink(update.Link)
578: 	previous, exists := states[snapshot.Index]
579: 
580: 	switch update.Header.Type {
581: 	case unix.RTM_DELLINK:
582: 		// A link that is not tracked was never reported, or its deletion was
583: 		// already reported when the links were listed again after a resubscribe.
584: 		if !exists {
585: 			return
586: 		}
587: 
588: 		delete(states, snapshot.Index)
589: 		delete(statsSamples, snapshot.Index)
590: 		w.emit(registry, "delete", previous)
591: 	case unix.RTM_NEWLINK:
592: 		// Query netem info for this interface
593: 		snapshot = withNetem(snapshot, netemQuery.Query())
594: 
595: 		if exists && snapshot.equal(previous) {
596: 			return
597: 		}
598: 
599: 		action := "create"
600: 		if exists {
601: 			action = "update"
602: 		}
603: 
604: 		states[snapshot.Index] = snapshot
605: 		if w.includeStats && statsSamples != nil {
606: 			if sample, ok := newStatsSample(snapshot, time.Now()); ok {
607: 				statsSamples[snapshot.Index] = sample
608: 			}
609: 		}
610: 		w.emit(registry, action, snapshot)
611: 	}
612: }
613: 
614: // pollNetemChanges checks for netem changes and emits update events.
615: func (w *netlinkWatcher) pollNetemChanges(
616: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
617: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
618: 	netemQuery *netemQuerier,
619: ) {
620: 	netemInfos := netemQuery.Query()
621: 
622: 	for idx, snapshot := range states {
623: 		previous := snapshot
624: 		info, hasNetem := netemInfos[idx]
625: 
626: 		if hasNetem {
627: 			snapshot.HasNetem = true
628: 			snapshot.Delay = info.Delay
629: 			snapshot.Jitter = info.Jitter
630: 			snapshot.PacketLoss = info.PacketLoss
631: 			snapshot.Rate = info.Rate
632: 			snapshot.Corruption = info.Corruption
633: 		} else if snapshot.HasNetem {
634: 			// Netem was removed
635: 			snapshot.HasNetem = false
636: 			snapshot.Delay = ""
637: 			snapshot.Jitter = ""
638: 			snapshot.PacketLoss = 0
639: 			snapshot.Rate = 0
640: 			snapshot.Corruption = 0
641: 		}
642: 
643: 		if !snapshot.equal(previous) {
644: 			states[idx] = snapshot
645: 			w.emit(registry, "update", snapshot)
646: 		}
647: 	}
648: }
649: 
650: // reportStitchMarks reports the links whose host-side stitch interface got
651: // marked after their last event. Only veths reported under their own name
652: // shortly before are looked at: the node's end of a veth-stitch link is a veth
653: // and is marked while its lab deploys.
654: func (w *netlinkWatcher) reportStitchMarks(
655: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
656: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
657: ) {
658: 	for idx, snapshot := range states {
659: 		reported, ok := w.reported[idx]
660: 		if !ok || snapshot.Type != "veth" ||
661: 			reported.attributes["ifname"] != snapshot.Name ||
662: 			time.Since(reported.since) > stitchMarkWindow {
663: 			continue
664: 		}
665: 
666: 		if interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)["ifname"] != snapshot.Name {
667: 			w.emit(registry, "update", snapshot)
668: 		}
669: 	}
670: }
671: 
672: // emit reports an event of a link. A deletion names the link as it was last
673: // reported. A link reported under another name before, such as the node's own
674: // interface until its stitch interface got marked, is first reported deleted
675: // under that name, so that clients do not keep both; it is then new to them.
676: func (w *netlinkWatcher) emit(registry *netlinkRegistry, action string, snapshot ifaceSnapshot) {
677: 	if w.container == nil {
678: 		return
679: 	}
680: 
681: 	if action == "delete" {
682: 		attributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)
683: 		if previous, ok := w.reported[snapshot.Index]; ok {
684: 			attributes = previous.attributes
685: 		}
686: 
687: 		delete(w.reported, snapshot.Index)
688: 		registry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, action, attributes, time.Now())
689: 
690: 		return
691: 	}
692: 
693: 	attributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)
694: 	if w.track(registry, snapshot.Index, attributes) && action == "update" {
695: 		action = "create"
696: 	}
697: 
698: 	registry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, action, attributes, time.Now())
699: }
700: 
701: // emitStats reports the counters of a link, under the name emit reports it.
702: func (w *netlinkWatcher) emitStats(
703: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
704: 	snapshot ifaceSnapshot,
705: 	metrics ifaceStatsMetrics,
706: ) {
707: 	if !registry.includeStats || w.container == nil || !snapshot.HasStats {
708: 		return
709: 	}
710: 
711: 	attributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)
712: 	if w.track(registry, snapshot.Index, attributes) {
713: 		registry.sendInterfaceEvent(
714: 			w.container,
715: 			"create",
716: 			maps.Clone(attributes),
717: 			metrics.Timestamp,
718: 		)
719: 	}
720: 
721: 	interfaceStatsAttributes(attributes, metrics)
722: 	registry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, "stats", attributes, metrics.Timestamp)
723: }
724: 
725: // track records what the link at index is reported as. When it was reported
726: // under another name before, it reports that name deleted and returns true.
727: func (w *netlinkWatcher) track(
728: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
729: 	index int,
730: 	attributes map[string]string,
731: ) bool {
732: 	if w.reported == nil {
733: 		w.reported = make(map[int]reportedInterface)
734: 	}
735: 
736: 	previous, known := w.reported[index]
737: 
738: 	since := time.Now()
739: 	if known {
740: 		since = previous.since
741: 	}
742: 
743: 	w.reported[index] = reportedInterface{attributes: maps.Clone(attributes), since: since}
744: 
745: 	if !known || previous.attributes["ifname"] == attributes["ifname"] {
746: 		return false
747: 	}
748: 
749: 	registry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, "delete", previous.attributes, time.Now())
750: 
751: 	return true
752: }
753: 
754: func firstContainerName(container *clabruntime.GenericContainer) string {
755: 	if container == nil || len(container.Names) == 0 {
756: 		return ""
757: 	}
758: 
759: 	return container.Names[0]
760: }
761: 
762: func containerLabel(container *clabruntime.GenericContainer) string {
763: 	if container == nil || container.Labels == nil {
764: 		return ""
765: 	}
766: 
767: 	return container.Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]
768: }
769: 
770: // interfaceSnapshotAttributes are the attributes every interface event reports for the
771: // interface itself.
772: func interfaceSnapshotAttributes(snapshot ifaceSnapshot) map[string]string {
773: 	attributes := map[string]string{
774: 		"ifname": snapshot.Name,
775: 		"index":  strconv.Itoa(snapshot.Index),
776: 		"mtu":    strconv.Itoa(snapshot.MTU),
777: 		"state":  snapshot.OperState,
778: 		"type":   snapshot.Type,
779: 	}
780: 
781: 	if snapshot.Alias != "" {
782: 		attributes["alias"] = snapshot.Alias
783: 	}
784: 
785: 	if snapshot.MAC != "" {
786: 		attributes["mac"] = snapshot.MAC
787: 	}
788: 
789: 	return attributes
790: }
791: 
792: // interfaceStatsAttributes adds the counters and rates of a stats event.
793: func interfaceStatsAttributes(attributes map[string]string, metrics ifaceStatsMetrics) {
794: 	attributes["rx_bytes"] = strconv.FormatUint(metrics.RxBytes, 10)
795: 	attributes["tx_bytes"] = strconv.FormatUint(metrics.TxBytes, 10)
796: 	attributes["rx_packets"] = strconv.FormatUint(metrics.RxPackets, 10)
797: 	attributes["tx_packets"] = strconv.FormatUint(metrics.TxPackets, 10)
798: 	attributes["rx_bps"] = strconv.FormatFloat(metrics.RxBps, 'f', -1, 64)
799: 	attributes["tx_bps"] = strconv.FormatFloat(metrics.TxBps, 'f', -1, 64)
800: 	attributes["rx_pps"] = strconv.FormatFloat(metrics.RxPps, 'f', -1, 64)
801: 	attributes["tx_pps"] = strconv.FormatFloat(metrics.TxPps, 'f', -1, 64)
802: 	attributes["interval_seconds"] = strconv.FormatFloat(metrics.Interval.Seconds(), 'f', -1, 64)
803: }
804: 
805: // labRuntimeInterfaceAttributes are the attributes of a lab runtime's interface event: those
806: // of the netlink interface events, for the node the runtime runs. Values the runtime could not
807: // read (no index or MTU without a shell in the node) are left out rather than reported as 0.
808: func labRuntimeInterfaceAttributes(
809: 	lab, name string,
810: 	iface *clablabruntime.InterfaceEventData,
811: ) map[string]string {
812: 	attributes := interfaceSnapshotAttributes(ifaceSnapshot{
813: 		Index:     iface.Index,
814: 		Name:      iface.Name,
815: 		Alias:     iface.Alias,
816: 		MTU:       iface.MTU,
817: 		MAC:       iface.MAC,
818: 		OperState: iface.OperState,
819: 		Type:      iface.Type,
820: 	})
821: 	if iface.Index <= 0 {
822: 		delete(attributes, "index")
823: 	}
824: 	if iface.MTU <= 0 {
825: 		delete(attributes, "mtu")
826: 	}
827: 
828: 	if lab != "" {
829: 		attributes["lab"] = lab
830: 	}
831: 	if name != "" {
832: 		attributes["name"] = name
833: 	}
834: 
835: 	if stats := iface.Stats; stats != nil {
836: 		interfaceStatsAttributes(attributes, ifaceStatsMetrics{
837: 			RxBytes:   stats.RxBytes,
838: 			TxBytes:   stats.TxBytes,
839: 			RxPackets: stats.RxPackets,
840: 			TxPackets: stats.TxPackets,
841: 			RxBps:     stats.RxBps,
842: 			TxBps:     stats.TxBps,
843: 			RxPps:     stats.RxPps,
844: 			TxPps:     stats.TxPps,
845: 			Interval:  stats.Interval,
846: 		})
847: 	}
848: 
849: 	return attributes
850: }
851: 
852: func interfaceAttributes(
853: 	container *clabruntime.GenericContainer,
854: 	snapshot ifaceSnapshot,
855: ) map[string]string {
856: 	attributes := interfaceSnapshotAttributes(snapshot)
857: 	attributes["origin"] = "netlink"
858: 
859: 	if lab := containerLabel(container); lab != "" {
860: 		attributes["lab"] = lab
861: 	}
862: 
863: 	if name := firstContainerName(container); name != "" {
864: 		attributes["name"] = name
865: 	}
866: 
867: 	overlayToolsInterface(container, attributes)
868: 
869: 	// Add netem attributes if present
870: 	if snapshot.HasNetem {
871: 		if snapshot.Delay != "" {
872: 			attributes["netem_delay"] = snapshot.Delay
873: 		}
874: 		if snapshot.Jitter != "" {
875: 			attributes["netem_jitter"] = snapshot.Jitter
876: 		}
877: 		if snapshot.PacketLoss != 0 {
878: 			attributes["netem_loss"] = strconv.FormatFloat(snapshot.PacketLoss, 'f', 2, 64) + "%"
879: 		}
880: 		if snapshot.Rate != 0 {
881: 			attributes["netem_rate"] = strconv.Itoa(snapshot.Rate) + "kbit"
882: 		}
883: 		if snapshot.Corruption != 0 {
884: 			attributes["netem_corruption"] = strconv.FormatFloat(
885: 				snapshot.Corruption,
886: 				'f',
887: 				2,
888: 				64,
889: 			) + "%"
890: 		}
891: 	}
892: 
893: 	return attributes
894: }
895: 
896: // overlayToolsInterface rewrites attributes to the host-side stitch interface
897: // when one exists, matching `inspect interfaces`.
898: func overlayToolsInterface(
899: 	container *clabruntime.GenericContainer,
900: 	attributes map[string]string,
901: ) {
902: 	if container == nil {
903: 		return
904: 	}
905: 
906: 	lab, node := clabcore.TopoIdentity(container.Labels)
907: 	if lab == "" || node == "" {
908: 		return
909: 	}
910: 
911: 	topoName := attributes["alias"]
912: 	if topoName == "" {
913: 		topoName = attributes["ifname"]
914: 	}
915: 
916: 	tools, ok := clablinks.ToolsInterface(lab, node, topoName)
917: 	if !ok {
918: 		return
919: 	}
920: 
921: 	snapshot := snapshotFromLink(tools)
922: 	attributes["ifname"] = snapshot.Name
923: 	attributes["alias"] = topoName
924: 	attributes["index"] = strconv.Itoa(snapshot.Index)
925: 	attributes["mtu"] = strconv.Itoa(snapshot.MTU)
926: 	attributes["state"] = snapshot.OperState
927: 	attributes["type"] = snapshot.Type
928: 
929: 	if snapshot.MAC != "" {
930: 		attributes["mac"] = snapshot.MAC
931: 	}
932: }

934: func (r *netlinkRegistry) sendInterfaceEvent(
935: 	container *clabruntime.GenericContainer,
936: 	action string,
937: 	attributes map[string]string,
938: 	timestamp time.Time,
939: ) {
940: 	event := aggregatedEvent{
941: 		Timestamp:   timestamp,
942: 		Type:        "interface",
943: 		Action:      action,
944: 		ActorID:     container.ShortID,
945: 		ActorName:   firstContainerName(container),
946: 		ActorFullID: container.ID,
947: 		Attributes:  attributes,
948: 	}
949: 
950: 	select {
951: 	case r.events <- event:
952: 	case <-r.ctx.Done():
953: 	}
954: }
955: 
956: func waitForNamespacePath(
957: 	ctx context.Context,
958: 	runtime clabruntime.ContainerRuntime,
959: 	containerID string,
960: ) (string, error) {
961: 	const (
962: 		attempts   = 5
963: 		retryDelay = 200 * time.Millisecond
964: 	)
965: 
966: 	var lastErr error
967: 
968: 	for i := 0; i < attempts; i++ {
969: 		nsPath, err := runtime.GetNSPath(ctx, containerID)
970: 		if err == nil && nsPath != "" {
971: 			return nsPath, nil
972: 		}
973: 
974: 		if err != nil {
975: 			lastErr = err
976: 		}
977: 
978: 		select {
979: 		case <-time.After(retryDelay):
980: 		case <-ctx.Done():
981: 			return "", ctx.Err()
982: 		}
983: 	}
984: 
985: 	if lastErr != nil {
986: 		return "", lastErr
987: 	}
988: 
989: 	return "", fmt.Errorf("namespace path not ready for container %s", containerID)
990: }
991: 
992: func snapshotInterfaces(netHandle *netlink.Handle) (map[int]ifaceSnapshot, error) {
993: 	if netHandle == nil {
994: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("netlink handle is nil")
995: 	}
996: 
997: 	links, err := netHandle.LinkList()
998: 	if err != nil {
999: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unable to list links: %w", err)
1000: 	}
1001: 
1002: 	states := make(map[int]ifaceSnapshot, len(links))
1003: 	for _, link := range links {
1004: 		snapshot := snapshotFromLink(link)
1005: 		states[snapshot.Index] = snapshot
1006: 	}
1007: 
1008: 	return states, nil
1009: }
1010: 
1011: // withNetem returns the snapshot with the netem settings of its interface.
1012: func withNetem(snapshot ifaceSnapshot, netemInfos map[int]netemInfo) ifaceSnapshot {
1013: 	if info, ok := netemInfos[snapshot.Index]; ok {
1014: 		snapshot.HasNetem = true
1015: 		snapshot.Delay = info.Delay
1016: 		snapshot.Jitter = info.Jitter
1017: 		snapshot.PacketLoss = info.PacketLoss
1018: 		snapshot.Rate = info.Rate
1019: 		snapshot.Corruption = info.Corruption
1020: 	}
1021: 
1022: 	return snapshot
1023: }
1024: 
1025: func snapshotFromLink(link netlink.Link) ifaceSnapshot {
1026: 	attrs := link.Attrs()
1027: 
1028: 	snapshot := ifaceSnapshot{
1029: 		Type: link.Type(),
1030: 	}
1031: 
1032: 	if attrs != nil {
1033: 		snapshot.Index = attrs.Index
1034: 		snapshot.Name = attrs.Name
1035: 		snapshot.Alias = attrs.Alias
1036: 		snapshot.MTU = attrs.MTU
1037: 		if len(attrs.HardwareAddr) > 0 {
1038: 			snapshot.MAC = attrs.HardwareAddr.String()
1039: 		}
1040: 		snapshot.OperState = attrs.OperState.String()
1041: 		if stats := attrs.Statistics; stats != nil {
1042: 			snapshot.HasStats = true
1043: 			snapshot.RxBytes = stats.RxBytes
1044: 			snapshot.TxBytes = stats.TxBytes
1045: 			snapshot.RxPackets = stats.RxPackets
1046: 			snapshot.TxPackets = stats.TxPackets
1047: 		}
1048: 	}
1049: 
1050: 	return snapshot
1051: }

1112: func newStatsSample(snapshot ifaceSnapshot, timestamp time.Time) (ifaceStatsSample, bool) {
1113: 	if !snapshot.HasStats {
1114: 		return ifaceStatsSample{}, false
1115: 	}
1116: 
1117: 	return ifaceStatsSample{
1118: 		RxBytes:   snapshot.RxBytes,
1119: 		TxBytes:   snapshot.TxBytes,
1120: 		RxPackets: snapshot.RxPackets,
1121: 		TxPackets: snapshot.TxPackets,
1122: 		Timestamp: timestamp,
1123: 	}, true
1124: }
1125: 
1126: func (w *netlinkWatcher) collectAndEmitStats(
1127: 	netHandle *netlink.Handle,
1128: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
1129: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
1130: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
1131: ) {
1132: 	if netHandle == nil || statsSamples == nil {
1133: 		return
1134: 	}
1135: 
1136: 	now := time.Now()
1137: 	// Fetch all interfaces with one netlink call per tick instead of one call
1138: 	// per interface to reduce syscall overhead in large labs.
1139: 	currentStates, err := snapshotInterfaces(netHandle)
1140: 	if err != nil {
1141: 		log.Debugf(
1142: 			"failed to collect interface stats snapshot for container %s: %v",
1143: 			firstContainerName(w.container),
1144: 			err,
1145: 		)
1146: 
1147: 		return
1148: 	}
1149: 
1150: 	for idx, state := range states {
1151: 		current, ok := currentStates[idx]
1152: 		if !ok {
1153: 			continue
1154: 		}
1155: 
1156: 		state.Name = current.Name
1157: 		state.Alias = current.Alias
1158: 		state.MTU = current.MTU
1159: 		state.MAC = current.MAC
1160: 		state.OperState = current.OperState
1161: 		state.Type = current.Type
1162: 		state.HasStats = current.HasStats
1163: 		state.RxBytes = current.RxBytes
1164: 		state.TxBytes = current.TxBytes
1165: 		state.RxPackets = current.RxPackets
1166: 		state.TxPackets = current.TxPackets
1167: 		states[idx] = state
1168: 
1169: 		if !state.HasStats {
1170: 			delete(statsSamples, idx)
1171: 
1172: 			continue
1173: 		}
1174: 
1175: 		prev, ok := statsSamples[idx]
1176: 		if !ok || now.Sub(prev.Timestamp) <= 0 {
1177: 			statsSamples[idx] = ifaceStatsSample{
1178: 				RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1179: 				TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1180: 				RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1181: 				TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1182: 				Timestamp: now,
1183: 			}
1184: 
1185: 			continue
1186: 		}
1187: 
1188: 		interval := now.Sub(prev.Timestamp)
1189: 		if interval <= 0 {
1190: 			statsSamples[idx] = ifaceStatsSample{
1191: 				RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1192: 				TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1193: 				RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1194: 				TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1195: 				Timestamp: now,
1196: 			}
1197: 
1198: 			continue
1199: 		}
1200: 
1201: 		rxBytesDelta := deltaCounter(prev.RxBytes, state.RxBytes)
1202: 		txBytesDelta := deltaCounter(prev.TxBytes, state.TxBytes)
1203: 		rxPacketsDelta := deltaCounter(prev.RxPackets, state.RxPackets)
1204: 		txPacketsDelta := deltaCounter(prev.TxPackets, state.TxPackets)
1205: 
1206: 		seconds := interval.Seconds()
1207: 		if seconds <= 0 {
1208: 			statsSamples[idx] = ifaceStatsSample{
1209: 				RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1210: 				TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1211: 				RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1212: 				TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1213: 				Timestamp: now,
1214: 			}
1215: 
1216: 			continue
1217: 		}
1218: 
1219: 		metrics := ifaceStatsMetrics{
1220: 			RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1221: 			TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1222: 			RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1223: 			TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1224: 			RxBps:     float64(rxBytesDelta) * 8 / seconds,
1225: 			TxBps:     float64(txBytesDelta) * 8 / seconds,
1226: 			RxPps:     float64(rxPacketsDelta) / seconds,
1227: 			TxPps:     float64(txPacketsDelta) / seconds,
1228: 			Interval:  interval,
1229: 			Timestamp: now,
1230: 		}
1231: 
1232: 		w.emitStats(registry, state, metrics)
1233: 
1234: 		statsSamples[idx] = ifaceStatsSample{
1235: 			RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1236: 			TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1237: 			RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1238: 			TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1239: 			Timestamp: now,
1240: 		}
1241: 	}
1242: }
1243: 
1244: func deltaCounter(previous, current uint64) uint64 {
1245: 	if current >= previous {
1246: 		return current - previous
1247: 	}
1248: 
1249: 	return current
1250: }

1253: 	msPerSec = 1000
1254: )

1267: func (q *netemQuerier) Close() {
1268: 	if q == nil || q.tcHandle == nil {
1269: 		return
1270: 	}
1271: 
1272: 	q.tcHandle.Close()
1273: 	q.tcHandle = nil
1274: }
1275: 
1276: // Query queries the TC qdiscs in the namespace and returns a map of
1277: // interface index to netem data.
1278: func (q *netemQuerier) Query() map[int]netemInfo {
1279: 	result := make(map[int]netemInfo)
1280: 
1281: 	qdiscs, err := q.qdiscs()
1282: 	if err != nil {
1283: 		log.Debugf("failed to query tc qdiscs: %v", err)
1284: 		return result
1285: 	}
1286: 
1287: 	for idx := range qdiscs {
1288: 		qdisc := &qdiscs[idx]
1289: 		if qdisc.Attribute.Kind != "netem" || qdisc.Netem == nil {
1290: 			continue
1291: 		}
1292: 
1293: 		info := netemInfoFromQdisc(qdisc)
1294: 		if info.hasValues() {
1295: 			result[int(qdisc.Ifindex)] = info
1296: 		}
1297: 	}
1298: 
1299: 	return result
1300: }
1301: 
1302: func (q *netemQuerier) qdiscs() ([]gotc.Object, error) {
1303: 	if q == nil {
1304: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("netem querier is nil")
1305: 	}
1306: 
1307: 	if err := q.ensureHandle(); err != nil {
1308: 		return nil, err
1309: 	}
1310: 
1311: 	qdiscs, err := clabnetem.Impairments(q.tcHandle)
1312: 	if err == nil {
1313: 		return qdiscs, nil
1314: 	}
1315: 
1316: 	// Retry once by reopening the tc socket in case it became stale.
1317: 	q.Close()
1318: 	if errOpen := q.ensureHandle(); errOpen != nil {
1319: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
1320: 			"failed to query tc qdiscs (%v), and failed to reopen tc socket (%v)",
1321: 			err,
1322: 			errOpen,
1323: 		)
1324: 	}
1325: 
1326: 	return clabnetem.Impairments(q.tcHandle)
1327: }
1328: 
1329: func (q *netemQuerier) ensureHandle() error {
1330: 	if q == nil {
1331: 		return fmt.Errorf("netem querier is nil")
1332: 	}
1333: 
1334: 	if q.tcHandle != nil {
1335: 		return nil
1336: 	}
1337: 
1338: 	tcnl, err := clabnetem.NewTC(int(q.nsHandle))
1339: 	if err != nil {
1340: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open tc socket for netem query: %w", err)
1341: 	}
1342: 
1343: 	q.tcHandle = tcnl
1344: 
1345: 	return nil
1346: }
1347: 
1348: type netemInfo struct {
1349: 	Delay      string
1350: 	Jitter     string
1351: 	PacketLoss float64
1352: 	Rate       int
1353: 	Corruption float64
1354: }
1355: 
1356: func (n netemInfo) hasValues() bool {
1357: 	return n.Delay != "" || n.Jitter != "" || n.PacketLoss != 0 || n.Rate != 0 || n.Corruption != 0
1358: }
1359: 
1360: func netemInfoFromQdisc(qdisc *gotc.Object) netemInfo {
1361: 	var info netemInfo
1362: 
1363: 	if qdisc.Netem == nil {
1364: 		return info
1365: 	}
1366: 
1367: 	if qdisc.Netem.Latency64 != nil && *qdisc.Netem.Latency64 != 0 {
1368: 		info.Delay = (time.Duration(*qdisc.Netem.Latency64) * time.Nanosecond).String()
1369: 	}
1370: 
1371: 	if qdisc.Netem.Jitter64 != nil && *qdisc.Netem.Jitter64 != 0 {
1372: 		info.Jitter = (time.Duration(*qdisc.Netem.Jitter64) * time.Nanosecond).String()
1373: 	}
1374: 
1375: 	if qdisc.Netem.Rate != nil && qdisc.Netem.Rate.Rate != 0 {
1376: 		info.Rate = int(qdisc.Netem.Rate.Rate * 8 / msPerSec)
1377: 	}
1378: 
1379: 	if qdisc.Netem.Corrupt != nil && qdisc.Netem.Corrupt.Probability != 0 {
1380: 		info.Corruption = math.Round((float64(qdisc.Netem.Corrupt.Probability)/
1381: 			float64(math.MaxUint32)*100)*100) / 100
1382: 	}
1383: 
1384: 	if qdisc.Netem.Qopt.Loss != 0 {
1385: 		info.PacketLoss = math.Round(
1386: 			(float64(qdisc.Netem.Qopt.Loss)/float64(math.MaxUint32)*100)*100) / 100
1387: 	}
1388: 
1389: 	return info
1390: }

\t\tclabruntime.EventActionKill:\n148: \t\tid := ev.ActorFullID\n149: \t\tif id == \"\" {\n150: \t\t\tid = ev.ActorID\n151: \t\t}\n152: \n153: \t\tr.Stop(id)\n154: \t}\n155: }\n156: \n157: func cloneContainer(container *clabruntime.GenericContainer) *clabruntime.GenericContainer {\n158: \tif container == nil {\n159: \t\treturn nil\n160: \t}\n161: \n162: \tclone := &clabruntime.GenericContainer{\n163: \t\tNames:   append([]string{}, container.Names...),\n164: \t\tID:      container.ID,\n165: \t\tShortID: container.ShortID,\n166: \t\tLabels:  cloneStringMap(container.Labels),\n167: \t}\n168: \n169: \tif clone.ShortID == \"\" {\n170: \t\tclone.ShortID = clabutils.ShortID(clone.ID)\n171: \t}\n172: \n173: \tif container.Runtime == nil {\n174: \t\treturn nil\n175: \t}\n176: \n177: \tclone.SetRuntime(container.Runtime)\n178: \n179: \treturn clone\n180: }\n181: \n182: func containerFromEvent(\n183: \truntime clabruntime.ContainerRuntime,\n184: \tev clabruntime.ContainerEvent,\n185: ) *clabruntime.GenericContainer {\n186: \tattributes := ev.Attributes\n187: \n188: \tname := ev.ActorName\n189: \tif name == \"\" && attributes != nil {\n190: \t\tname = attributes[\"name\"]\n191: \t}\n192: \n193: \tid := ev.ActorFullID\n194: \tif id == \"\" {\n195: \t\tid = ev.ActorID\n196: \t}\n197: \n198: \tif id == \"\" &clabruntime.GenericContainer{\n208: \t\tID:      id,\n209: \t\tShortID: clabutils.ShortID(short),\n210: \t}\n211: \n212: \tif name != \"\" {\n213: \t\tcontainer.Names = []string{name}\n214: \t}\n215: \n216: \tif attributes != nil {\n217: \t\tfor _, key := range []string{\n218: \t\t\tclabconstants.Containerlab,\n219: \t\t\tclabconstants.NodeName,\n220: \t\t\tclabconstants.RootNodeName,\n221: \t\t} \t\t\t\tcontainer.Labels[key] = v\n228: \t\t\t}\n229: \t\t}\n230: \t}\n231: \n232: \tif container.ShortID == \"\" {\n233: \t\tcontainer.ShortID = clabutils.ShortID(container.ID)\n234: \t}\n235: \n236: \tif runtime != nil {\n237: \t\tcontainer.SetRuntime(runtime)\n238: \t}\n239: \n240: \treturn container\n241: }\n242: \n243: type netlinkWatcher struct {\n244: \tcontainer       *clabruntime.GenericContainer\n245: \tcancel          context.CancelFunc\n246: \tdone            chan struct{}\n247: \tincludeSnapshot bool\n248: \tincludeStats    bool\n249: \tstatsInterval   time.Duration\n250: \tnsHandle        netns.NsHandle\n251: \t// reported holds what each link was last reported as, by its index in the\n252: \t// container's namespace.\n253: \treported map[int]reportedInterface\n254: }\n255: \n256: // reportedInterface is what a link was last reported as.\n257: type reportedInterface struct {\n258: \tattributes map[string]string\n259: \t// since is when the link was first reported.\n260: \tsince time.Time\n261: }\n262: \n263: // stitchMarkWindow is how long after a link is first reported its host-side\n264: // stitch interface is looked for while the link itself does not change.\n265: // Deploying a veth-stitch link marks that interface right after it creates the\n266: // link, without a change in the node's namespace.\n267: var stitchMarkWindow = time.Minute\n268: \n269: // Retry settings for a watcher that could not subscribe to link updates.\n270: var (\n271: \twatchMaxAttempts   = 5\n272: \twatchRetryDelay    = time.Second\n273: \twatchMaxRetryDelay = 10 * time.Second\n274: )\n275: \n276: func (w *netlinkWatcher) run(ctx context.Context, registry *netlinkRegistry) {\n277: \tdefer close(w.done)\n278: \tif w.container == nil {\n279: \t\treturn\n280: \t}\n281: \n282: \tdefer registry.remove(w.container.ID, w)\n283: \n284: \tcontainerName := firstContainerName(w.container)\n285: \tif w.container.Runtime == nil {\n286: \t\tlog.Debugf(\"container %s has no runtime, skipping netlink watcher\", containerName)\n287: \n288: \t\treturn\n289: \t}\n290: \n291: \t// The states outlive a subscription, so a resubscribe only reports what\n292: \t// changed while no subscription was active.\n293: \tstates := make(map[int]ifaceSnapshot)\n294: \n295: \tvar statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample\n296: \tif w.includeStats {\n297: \t\tstatsSamples = make(map[int]ifaceStatsSample)\n298: \t}\n299: \n300: \tinitial := true\n301: \tfailures := 0\n302: \tdelay := watchRetryDelay\n303: \n304: \tfor {\n305: \t\tsubscribed, err := w.watch(ctx, registry, states, statsSamples, initial)\n306: \t\tif ctx.Err() != nil {\n307: \t\t\treturn\n308: \t\t}\n309: \n310: \t\tif subscribed {\n311: \t\t\tinitial = false\n312: \t\t\tfailures = 0\n313: \t\t\tdelay = watchRetryDelay\n314: \n315: \t\t\tlog.Warnf(\n316: \t\t\t\t\"netlink subscription for container %s ended, resubscribing: %v\",\n317: \t\t\t\tcontainerName,\n318: \t\t\t\terr,\n319: \t\t\t)\n320: \t\t} else {\n321: \t\t\tfailures++\n322: \t\t\tif failures >= watchMaxAttempts {\n323: \t\t\t\tlog.Warnf(\"stopped watching interfaces of container %s: %v\", containerName, err)\n324: \n325: \t\t\t\treturn\n326: \t\t\t}\n327: \n328: \t\t\tlog.Debugf(\n329: \t\t\t\t\"failed to watch interfaces of container %s, retrying in %s: %v\",\n330: \t\t\t\tcontainerName,\n331: \t\t\t\tdelay,\n332: \t\t\t\terr,\n333: \t\t\t)\n334: \t\t}\n335: \n336: \t\tselect {\n337: \t\tcase <-time.After(delay):\n338: \t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n339: \t\t\treturn\n340: \t\t}\n341: \n342: \t\tdelay = min(delay*2, watchMaxRetryDelay)\n343: \t}\n344: }\n345: \n346: // watch subscribes to link updates in the container's namespace and handles\n347: // them until ctx ends or the subscription fails. It reports whether it\n348: // subscribed. The subscription lists the existing links itself, so no change\n349: // between listing and subscribing is lost.\n350: func (w *netlinkWatcher) watch(\n351: \tctx context.Context,\n352: \tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n353: \tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n354: \tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n355: \tinitial bool,\n356: ) (bool, error) {\n357: \tnsPath, err := waitForNamespacePath(ctx, w.container.Runtime, w.container.ID)\n358: \tif err != nil {\n359: \t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to resolve netns: %w\", err)\n360: \t}\n361: \n362: \tnsHandle, err := netns.GetFromPath(nsPath)\n363: \tif err != nil {\n364: \t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open netns: %w\", err)\n365: \t}\n366: \tdefer nsHandle.Close()\n367: \n368: \tw.nsHandle = nsHandle\n369: \n370: \tnetHandle, err := netlink.NewHandleAt(nsHandle)\n371: \tif err != nil {\n372: \t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create netlink handle: %w\", err)\n373: \t}\n374: \tdefer netHandle.Close()\n375: \n376: \tnetemQuery := newNetemQuerier(nsHandle)\n377: \tdefer netemQuery.Close()\n378: \n379: \tnetemInfos := netemQuery.Query()\n380: \n381: \tupdates := make(chan netlink.LinkUpdate, 32)\n382: \tdone := make(chan struct{})\n383: \tdefer close(done)\n384: \n385: \tvar (\n386: \t\terrMu     sync.Mutex\n387: \t\tlastError error\n388: \t)\n389: \n390: \topts := netlink.LinkSubscribeOptions{\n391: \t\tNamespace:    &nsHandle,\n392: \t\tListExisting: true,\n393: \t\t// An interrupted listing is reported here too. It needs no action:\n394: \t\t// links that changed during the listing also arrive as updates.\n395: \t\tErrorCallback: func(err error) {\n396: \t\t\tlog.Debugf(\n397: \t\t\t\t\"netlink subscription for container %s: %v\",\n398: \t\t\t\tfirstContainerName(w.container),\n399: \t\t\t\terr,\n400: \t\t\t)\n401: \n402: \t\t\terrMu.Lock()\n403: \t\t\tlastError = err\n404: \t\t\terrMu.Unlock()\n405: \t\t},\n406: \t}\n407: \n408: \tif err := netlink.LinkSubscribeWithOptions(updates, done, opts); err != nil {\n409: \t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to subscribe to netlink updates: %w\", err)\n410: \t}\n411: \n412: \tvar vanished map[int]struct{}\n413: \tif len(states) > 0 {\n414: \t\tvanished = w.removeVanishedLinks(netHandle, states, statsSamples, registry)\n415: \t}\n416: \n417: \t// Set up netem polling interval (reuse stats interval or default to 1s).\n418: \tnetemPollInterval := w.statsInterval\n419: \tif netemPollInterval == 0 {\n420: \t\tnetemPollInterval = time.Second\n421: \t}\n422: \tnetemTicker := time.NewTicker(netemPollInterval)\n423: \tdefer netemTicker.Stop()\n424: \n425: \tvar (\n426: \t\tticker  *time.Ticker\n427: \t\ttickerC <-chan time.Time\n428: \t)\n429: \tif w.includeStats && w.statsInterval > 0 {\n430: \t\tticker = time.NewTicker(w.statsInterval)\n431: \t\ttickerC = ticker.C\n432: \t\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n433: \t}\n434: \n435: \tfor {\n436: \t\tselect {\n437: \t\tcase <-tickerC:\n438: \t\t\tw.collectAndEmitStats(netHandle, states, statsSamples, registry)\n439: \t\tcase <-netemTicker.C:\n440: \t\t\tw.pollNetemChanges(states, registry, netemQuery)\n441: \t\t\tw.reportStitchMarks(states, registry)\n442: \t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n443: \t\t\treturn true, nil\n444: \t\tcase update, ok := <-updates:\n445: \t\t\tif !ok {\n446: \t\t\t\terrMu.Lock()\n447: \t\t\t\terr := lastError\n448: \t\t\t\terrMu.Unlock()\n449: \n450: \t\t\t\tif err == nil {\n451: \t\t\t\t\terr = errors.New(\"update channel closed\")\n452: \t\t\t\t}\n453: \n454: \t\t\t\treturn true, err\n455: \t\t\t}\n456: \n457: \t\t\t// Replies to the listing carry NLM_F_MULTI, notifications do not.\n458: \t\t\tif update.Header.Flags&unix.NLM_F_MULTI != 0 {\n459: \t\t\t\tw.processExisting(\n460: \t\t\t\t\tstates,\n461: \t\t\t\t\tstatsSamples,\n462: \t\t\t\t\tupdate.Link,\n463: \t\t\t\t\tnetemInfos,\n464: \t\t\t\t\tvanished,\n465: \t\t\t\t\tregistry,\n466: \t\t\t\t\tinitial,\n467: \t\t\t\t)\n468: \n469: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n470: \t\t\t}\n471: \n472: \t\t\tw.processUpdate(states, statsSamples, update, registry, netemQuery)\n473: \t\t}\n474: \t}\n475: }\n476: \n477: // processExisting records a link listed when the subscription started. On the\n478: // first subscription a new link is reported as a snapshot when requested; after\n479: // a resubscribe it is reported as created.\n480: //\n481: // The listing can be older than the check in removeVanishedLinks, so it can\n482: // still hold a link that check reported as deleted. Such a link is skipped; its\n483: // deletion notification follows and is ignored, as the link is not tracked.\n484: func (w *netlinkWatcher) processExisting(\n485: \tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n486: \tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n487: \tlink netlink.Link,\n488: \tnetemInfos map[int]netemInfo,\n489: \tvanished map[int]struct{},\n490: \tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n491: \tinitial bool,\n492: ) {\n493: \tif link == nil || link.Attrs() == nil {\n494: \t\treturn\n495: \t}\n496: \n497: \tif _, ok := vanished[link.Attrs().Index]; ok {\n498: \t\treturn\n499: \t}\n500: \n501: \tsnapshot := withNetem(snapshotFromLink(link), netemInfos)\n502: \tprevious, exists := states[snapshot.Index]\n503: \n504: \tif exists && snapshot.equal(previous) {\n505: \t\treturn\n506: \t}\n507: \n508: \tstates[snapshot.Index] = snapshot\n509: \tif statsSamples != nil {\n510: \t\tif sample, ok := newStatsSample(snapshot, time.Now()); ok {\n511: \t\t\tstatsSamples[snapshot.Index] = sample\n512: \t\t}\n513: \t}\n514: \n515: \tswitch {\n516: \tcase exists:\n517: \t\tw.emit(registry, \"update\", snapshot)\n518: \tcase !initial:\n519: \t\tw.emit(registry, \"create\", snapshot)\n520: \tcase w.includeSnapshot:\n521: \t\tw.emit(registry, \"snapshot\", snapshot)\n522: \t}\n523: }\n524: \n525: // removeVanishedLinks reports the links that were deleted while no\n526: // subscription was active and returns their indexes.\n527: func (w *netlinkWatcher) removeVanishedLinks(\n528: \tnetHandle *netlink.Handle,\n529: \tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n530: \tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n531: \tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n532: ) map[int]struct{} {\n533: \tcurrent, err := snapshotInterfaces(netHandle)\n534: \tif err != nil {\n535: \t\tlog.Debugf(\n536: \t\t\t\"failed to list interfaces of container %s: %v\",\n537: \t\t\tfirstContainerName(w.container),\n538: \t\t\terr,\n539: \t\t)\n540: \n541: \t\treturn nil\n542: \t}\n543: \n544: \tvanished := map[int]struct{}{}\n545: \n546: \tfor idx, snapshot := range states {\n547: \t\tif _, ok := current[idx]; ok {\n548: \t\t\tcontinue\n549: \t\t}\n550: \n551: \t\tvanished[idx] = struct{}{}\n552: \n553: \t\tdelete(states, idx)\n554: \t\tdelete(statsSamples, idx)\n555: \t\tw.emit(registry, \"delete\", snapshot)\n556: \t}\n557: \n558: \treturn vanished\n559: }\n560: \n561: func (w *netlinkWatcher) processUpdate(\n562: \tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n563: \tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n564: \tupdate netlink.LinkUpdate,\n565: \tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n566: \tnetemQuery *netemQuerier,\n567: ) {\n568: \tif update.Link == nil {\n569: \t\treturn\n570: \t}\n571: \n572: \tattrs := update.Link.Attrs()\n573: \tif attrs == nil {\n574: \t\treturn\n575: \t}\n576: \n577: \tsnapshot := snapshotFromLink(update.Link)\n578: \tprevious, exists := states[snapshot.Index]\n579: \n580: \tswitch update.Header.Type {\n581: \tcase unix.RTM_DELLINK:\n582: \t\t// A link that is not tracked was never reported, or its deletion was\n583: \t\t// already reported when the links were listed again after a resubscribe.\n584: \t\tif !exists {\n585: \t\t\treturn\n586: \t\t}\n587: \n588: \t\tdelete(states, snapshot.Index)\n589: \t\tdelete(statsSamples, snapshot.Index)\n590: \t\tw.emit(registry, \"delete\", previous)\n591: \tcase unix.RTM_NEWLINK:\n592: \t\t// Query netem info for this interface\n593: \t\tsnapshot = withNetem(snapshot, netemQuery.Query())\n594: \n595: \t\tif exists && snapshot.equal(previous) {\n596: \t\t\treturn\n597: \t\t}\n598: \n599: \t\taction := \"create\"\n600: \t\tif exists {\n601: \t\t\taction = \"update\"\n602: \t\t}\n603: \n604: \t\tstates[snapshot.Index] = snapshot\n605: \t\tif w.includeStats && statsSamples != nil {\n606: \t\t\tif sample, ok := newStatsSample(snapshot, time.Now()); ok {\n607: \t\t\t\tstatsSamples[snapshot.Index] = sample\n608: \t\t\t}\n609: \t\t}\n610: \t\tw.emit(registry, action, snapshot)\n611: \t}\n612: }\n613: \n614: // pollNetemChanges checks for netem changes and emits update events.\n615: func (w *netlinkWatcher) pollNetemChanges(\n616: \tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n617: \tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n618: \tnetemQuery *netemQuerier,\n619: ) {\n620: \tnetemInfos := netemQuery.Query()\n621: \n622: \tfor idx, snapshot := range states {\n623: \t\tprevious := snapshot\n624: \t\tinfo, hasNetem := netemInfos[idx]\n625: \n626: \t\tif hasNetem {\n627: \t\t\tsnapshot.HasNetem = true\n628: \t\t\tsnapshot.Delay = info.Delay\n629: \t\t\tsnapshot.Jitter = info.Jitter\n630: \t\t\tsnapshot.PacketLoss = info.PacketLoss\n631: \t\t\tsnapshot.Rate = info.Rate\n632: \t\t\tsnapshot.Corruption = info.Corruption\n633: \t\t} else if snapshot.HasNetem {\n634: \t\t\t// Netem was removed\n635: \t\t\tsnapshot.HasNetem = false\n636: \t\t\tsnapshot.Delay = \"\"\n637: \t\t\tsnapshot.Jitter = \"\"\n638: \t\t\tsnapshot.PacketLoss = 0\n639: \t\t\tsnapshot.Rate = 0\n640: \t\t\tsnapshot.Corruption = 0\n641: \t\t}\n642: \n643: \t\tif !snapshot.equal(previous) {\n644: \t\t\tstates[idx] = snapshot\n645: \t\t\tw.emit(registry, \"update\", snapshot)\n646: \t\t}\n647: \t}\n648: }\n649: \n650: // reportStitchMarks reports the links whose host-side stitch interface got\n651: // marked after their last event. Only veths reported under their own name\n652: // shortly before are looked at: the node's end of a veth-stitch link is a veth\n653: // and is marked while its lab deploys.\n654: func (w *netlinkWatcher) reportStitchMarks(\n655: \tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n656: \tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n657: ) {\n658: \tfor idx, snapshot := range states {\n659: \t\treported, ok := w.reported[idx]\n660: \t\tif !ok || snapshot.Type != \"veth\" ||\n661: \t\t\treported.attributes[\"ifname\"] != snapshot.Name ||\n662: \t\t\ttime.Since(reported.since) > stitchMarkWindow {\n663: \t\t\tcontinue\n664: \t\t}\n665: \n666: \t\tif interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)[\"ifname\"] != snapshot.Name {\n667: \t\t\tw.emit(registry, \"update\", snapshot)\n668: \t\t}\n669: \t}\n670: }\n671: \n672: // emit reports an event of a link. A deletion names the link as it was last\n673: // reported. A link reported under another name before, such as the node's own\n674: // interface until its stitch interface got marked, is first reported deleted\n675: // under that name, so that clients do not keep both; it is then new to them.\n676: func (w *netlinkWatcher) emit(registry *netlinkRegistry, action string, snapshot ifaceSnapshot) {\n677: \tif w.container == nil {\n678: \t\treturn\n679: \t}\n680: \n681: \tif action == \"delete\" {\n682: \t\tattributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)\n683: \t\tif previous, ok := w.reported[snapshot.Index]; ok {\n684: \t\t\tattributes = previous.attributes\n685: \t\t}\n686: \n687: \t\tdelete(w.reported, snapshot.Index)\n688: \t\tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, action, attributes, time.Now())\n689: \n690: \t\treturn\n691: \t}\n692: \n693: \tattributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)\n694: \tif w.track(registry, snapshot.Index, attributes) && action == \"update\" {\n695: \t\taction = \"create\"\n696: \t}\n697: \n698: \tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, action, attributes, time.Now())\n699: }\n700: \n701: // emitStats reports the counters of a link, under the name emit reports it.\n702: func (w *netlinkWatcher) emitStats(\n703: \tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n704: \tsnapshot ifaceSnapshot,\n705: \tmetrics ifaceStatsMetrics,\n706: ) {\n707: \tif !registry.includeStats || w.container == nil || !snapshot.HasStats {\n708: \t\treturn\n709: \t}\n710: \n711: \tattributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)\n712: \tif w.track(registry, snapshot.Index, attributes) {\n713: \t\tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(\n714: \t\t\tw.container,\n715: \t\t\t\"create\",\n716: \t\t\tmaps.Clone(attributes),\n717: \t\t\tmetrics.Timestamp,\n718: \t\t)\n719: \t}\n720: \n721: \tinterfaceStatsAttributes(attributes, metrics)\n722: \tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"stats\", attributes, metrics.Timestamp)\n723: }\n724: \n725: // track records what the link at index is reported as. When it was reported\n726: // under another name before, it reports that name deleted and returns true.\n727: func (w *netlinkWatcher) track(\n728: \tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n729: \tindex int,\n730: \tattributes map[string]string,\n731: ) bool {\n732: \tif w.reported == nil {\n733: \t\tw.reported = make(map[int]reportedInterface)\n734: \t}\n735: \n736: \tprevious, known := w.reported[index]\n737: \n738: \tsince := time.Now()\n739: \tif known {\n740: \t\tsince = previous.since\n741: \t}\n742: \n743: \tw.reported[index] = reportedInterface{attributes: maps.Clone(attributes), since: since}\n744: \n745: \tif !known || previous.attributes[\"ifname\"] == attributes[\"ifname\"] {\n746: \t\treturn false\n747: \t}\n748: \n749: \tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"delete\", previous.attributes, time.Now())\n750: \n751: \treturn true\n752: }\n753: \n754: func firstContainerName(container *clabruntime.GenericContainer) string {\n755: \tif container == nil || len(container.Names) == 0 {\n756: \t\treturn \"\"\n757: \t}\n758: \n759: \treturn container.Names[0]\n760: }\n761: \n762: func containerLabel(container *clabruntime.GenericContainer) string {\n763: \tif container == nil || container.Labels == nil {\n764: \t\treturn \"\"\n765: \t}\n766: \n767: \treturn container.Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]\n768: }\n769: \n770: // interfaceSnapshotAttributes are the attributes every interface event reports for the\n771: // interface itself.\n772: func interfaceSnapshotAttributes(snapshot ifaceSnapshot) map[string]string {\n773: \tattributes := map[string]string{\n774: \t\t\"ifname\": snapshot.Name,\n775: \t\t\"index\":  strconv.Itoa(snapshot.Index),\n776: \t\t\"mtu\":    strconv.Itoa(snapshot.MTU),\n777: \t\t\"state\":  snapshot.OperState,\n778: \t\t\"type\":   snapshot.Type,\n779: \t}\n780: \n781: \tif snapshot.Alias != \"\" {\n782: \t\tattributes[\"alias\"] = snapshot.Alias\n783: \t}\n784: \n785: \tif snapshot.MAC != \"\" {\n786: \t\tattributes[\"mac\"] = snapshot.MAC\n787: \t}\n788: \n789: The subscription lists the existing links itself, so no change\n// between listing and subscribing is lost.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) watch(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n\tinitial bool,\n) (bool, error) {\n\tnsPath, err := waitForNamespacePath(ctx, w.container.Runtime, w.container.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to resolve netns: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnsHandle, err := netns.GetFromPath(nsPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open netns: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer nsHandle.Close()\n\n\tw.nsHandle = nsHandle\n\n\tnetHandle, err := netlink.NewHandleAt(nsHandle)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create netlink handle: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer netHandle.Close()\n\n\tnetemQuery := newNetemQuerier(nsHandle)\n\tdefer netemQuery.Close()\n\n\tnetemInfos := netemQuery.Query()\n\n\tupdates := make(chan netlink.LinkUpdate, 32)\n\tdone := make(chan struct{})\n\tdefer close(done)\n\n\tvar (\n\t\terrMu     sync.Mutex\n\t\tlastError error\n\t)\n\n\topts := netlink.LinkSubscribeOptions{\n\t\tNamespace:    &nsHandle,\n\t\tListExisting: true,\n\t\t// An interrupted listing is reported here too. On the\n// first subscription a new link is reported as a snapshot when requested; after\n// a resubscribe it is reported as created.\n//\n// The listing can be older than the check in removeVanishedLinks, so it can\n// still hold a link that check reported as deleted. Such a link is skipped; its\n// deletion notification follows and is ignored, as the link is not tracked.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) processExisting(\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n\tlink netlink.Link,\n\tnetemInfos map[int]netemInfo,\n\tvanished map[int]struct{},\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tinitial bool,\n) {\n\tif link == nil || link.Attrs() == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif _, ok := vanished[link.Attrs().Index]; ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvanished[idx] = struct{}{}\n\n\t\tdelete(states, idx)\n\t\tdelete(statsSamples, idx)\n\t\tw.emit(registry, \"delete\", snapshot)\n\t}\n\n\treturn vanished\n}\n\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) processUpdate(\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n\tupdate netlink.LinkUpdate,\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tnetemQuery *netemQuerier,\n) {\n\tif update.Link == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tattrs := update.Link.Attrs()\n\tif attrs == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsnapshot := snapshotFromLink(update.Link)\n\tprevious, exists := states[snapshot.Index]\n\n\tswitch update.Header.Type {\n\tcase unix.RTM_DELLINK:\n\t\t// A link that is not tracked was never reported, or its deletion was\n\t\t// already reported when the links were listed again after a resubscribe.\n\t\tif !exists {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tdelete(states, snapshot.Index)\n\t\tdelete(statsSamples, snapshot.Index)\n\t\tw.emit(registry, \"delete\", previous)\n\tcase unix.RTM_NEWLINK:\n\t\t// Query netem info for this interface\n\t\tsnapshot = withNetem(snapshot, netemQuery.Query())\n\n\t\tif exists && snapshot.equal(previous) {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\taction := \"create\"\n\t\tif exists {\n\t\t\taction = \"update\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tstates[snapshot.Index] = snapshot\n\t\tif w.includeStats && statsSamples != nil {\n\t\t\tif sample, ok := newStatsSample(snapshot, time.Now()); Only veths reported under their own name\n// shortly before are looked at: the node's end of a veth-stitch link is a veth\n// and is marked while its lab deploys.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) reportStitchMarks(\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n) {\n\tfor idx, snapshot := range states {\n\t\treported, ok := w.reported[idx]\n\t\tif !ok || snapshot.Type != \"veth\" ||\n\t\t\treported.attributes[\"ifname\"] != snapshot.Name ||\n\t\t\ttime.Since(reported.since) > stitchMarkWindow {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)[\"ifname\"] != snapshot.Name {\n\t\t\tw.emit(registry, \"update\", snapshot)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// emit reports an event of a link. A deletion names the link as it was last\n// reported. A link reported under another name before, such as the node's own\n// interface until its stitch interface got marked, is first reported deleted\n// under that name, so that clients do not keep both; it is then new to them.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) emit(registry *netlinkRegistry, action string, snapshot ifaceSnapshot) {\n\tif w.container == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif action == \"delete\" {\n\t\tattributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)\n\t\tif previous, ok := w.reported[snapshot.Index]; ok {\n\t\t\tattributes = previous.attributes\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tdelete(w.reported, snapshot.Index)\n\t\tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, action, attributes, time.Now())\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tattributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)\n\tif w.track(registry, snapshot.Index, attributes) && action == \"update\" {\n\t\taction = \"create\"\n\t}\n\n\tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, action, attributes, time.Now())\n}\n\n// emitStats reports the counters of a link, under the name emit reports it.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) emitStats(\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tsnapshot ifaceSnapshot,\n\tmetrics ifaceStatsMetrics,\n) {\n\tif !registry.includeStats || w.container == nil || !snapshot.HasStats {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tattributes := interfaceAttributes(w.container, snapshot)\n\tif w.track(registry, snapshot.Index, attributes) {\n\t\tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(\n\t\t\tw.container,\n\t\t\t\"create\",\n\t\t\tmaps.Clone(attributes),\n\t\t\tmetrics.Timestamp,\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\tinterfaceStatsAttributes(attributes, metrics)\n\tregistry.sendInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"stats\", attributes, metrics.Timestamp)\n}\n\n// track records what the link at index is reported as. When it was reported\n// under another name before, it reports that name deleted and returns true.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) track(\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tindex int,\n\tattributes 22: // eventLog collects the interface events of a registry by interface name.
23: type eventLog struct {
24: 	mu      sync.Mutex
25: 	actions map[string][]string
26: }
27: 
28: func collectEvents(ctx context.Context, events <-chan aggregatedEvent) *eventLog {
29: 	l := &eventLog{actions: map[string][]string{}}
30: 
31: 	go func() {
32: 		for {
33: 			select {
34: 			case ev := <-events:
35: 				l.mu.Lock()
36: 				name := ev.Attributes["ifname"]
37: 				l.actions[name] = append(l.actions[name], ev.Action)
38: 				l.mu.Unlock()
39: 			case <-ctx.Done():
40: 				return
41: 			}
42: 		}
43: 	}()
44: 
45: 	return l
46: }
47: 
48: func (l *eventLog) get(name string) []string {
49: 	l.mu.Lock()
50: 	defer l.mu.Unlock()
51: 
52: 	return slices.Clone(l.actions[name])
53: }
54: 
55: // waitFor waits until every name has an event with one of the actions.
56: func (l *eventLog) waitFor(t *testing.T, names []string, actions ...string) {
57: 	t.Helper()
58: 
59: 	deadline := time.Now().Add(3 * time.Second)
60: 	for {
61: 		var missing []string
62: 		for _, name := range names {
63: 			if !slices.ContainsFunc(l.get(name), func(a string) bool {
64: 				return slices.Contains(actions, a)
65: 			}) {
66: 				missing = append(missing, name)
67: 			}
68: 		}
69: 
70: 		if len(missing) == 0 {
71: 			return
72: 		}
73: 
74: 		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
75: 			t.Fatalf("no %v event for %v", actions, missing)
76: 		}
77: 
78: 		time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
79: 	}
80: }
81: 
82: // newTestNetns creates a named network namespace without leaving the
83: // current thread in it.
84: func newTestNetns(t *testing.T) (string, *netlink.Handle) {
85: 	t.Helper()
86: 
87: 	if os.Geteuid() != 0 {
88: 		t.Skip("requires root to create network namespaces")
89: 	}
90: 
91: 	runtime.LockOSThread()
92: 	defer runtime.UnlockOSThread()
93: 
94: 	orig, err := netns.Get()
95: 	if err != nil {
96: 		t.Fatalf("failed to get current netns: %v", err)
97: 	}
98: 	defer orig.Close()
99: 
100: 	name := fmt.Sprintf("clabtst-ev-%d-%d", os.Getpid(), time.Now().UnixNano())
101: 
102: 	ns, err := netns.NewNamed(name)
103: 	if err != nil {
104: 		t.Fatalf("failed to create netns: %v", err)
105: 	}
106: 
107: 	if err := netns.Set(orig); err != nil {
108: 		t.Fatalf("failed to return to the original netns: %v", err)
109: 	}
110: 
111: 	handle, err := netlink.NewHandleAt(ns)
112: 	if err != nil {
113: 		t.Fatalf("failed to create netlink handle: %v", err)
114: 	}
115: 
116: 	t.Cleanup(func() {
117: 		handle.Close()
118: 		ns.Close()
119: 		_ = netns.DeleteNamed(name)
120: 	})
121: 
122: 	return "/run/netns/" + name, handle
123: }
124: 
125: func addDummy(t *testing.T, handle *netlink.Handle, name string) {
126: 	t.Helper()
127: 
128: 	err := handle.LinkAdd(&netlink.Dummy{LinkAttrs: netlink.LinkAttrs{Name: name}})
129: 	if err != nil {
130: 		t.Errorf("failed to add %s: %v", name, err)
131: 	}
132: }

134: func newTestContainer(
135: 	t *testing.T,
136: 	getNSPath func() (string, error),
137: ) *clabruntime.GenericContainer {
138: 	t.Helper()

143: 	).AnyTimes()

155: func startRegistry(
156: 	t *testing.T,
157: 	container *clabruntime.GenericContainer,
158: ) (*netlinkRegistry, *eventLog) {
159: 	t.Helper()
160: 
161: 	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
162: 	events := make(chan aggregatedEvent, 1024)
163: 	registry := newNetlinkRegistry(ctx, events, true, false, 0)
164: 	collected := collectEvents(ctx, events)
165: 
166: 	registry.Start(container)
167: 	t.Cleanup(func() {
168: 		registry.Stop(container.ID)
169: 		cancel()
170: 	})
171: 
172: 	return registry, collected
173: }

246: 	)

270: 	t.Cleanup(func() { watchMaxAttempts, watchRetryDelay = origAttempts, origDelay })

286: // TestProcessExistingAfterResubscribe checks how a listed link is reported on
287: // the first subscription and after a resubscribe.
288: func TestProcessExistingAfterResubscribe(t *testing.T) {
289: 	link := func(mtu int) netlink.Link {
290: 		return &netlink.Dummy{LinkAttrs: netlink.LinkAttrs{Index: 7, Name: "eth1", MTU: mtu}}
291: 	}
292: 
293: 	tests := map[string]struct {
294: 		includeSnapshot bool
295: 		initial         bool
296: 		known           netlink.Link
297: 		vanished        bool
298: 		want            []string
299: 	}{
300: 		"first subscription": {
301: 			includeSnapshot: true,
302: 			initial:         true,
303: 			want:            []string{"snapshot"},
304: 		},
305: 		"first subscription without snapshot": {initial: true},
306: 		"new after resubscribe":               {want: []string{"create"}},
307: 		"changed after resubscribe":           {known: link(1400), want: []string{"update"}},
308: 		"unchanged after resubscribe":         {known: link(1500)},
309: 		"deleted while resubscribing":         {vanished: true},
310: 	}
311: 
312: 	for name, tt := range tests {
313: 		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
314: 			events := make(chan aggregatedEvent, 8)
315: 			registry := newNetlinkRegistry(t.Context(), events, tt.includeSnapshot, false, 0)
316: 			watcher := &netlinkWatcher{
317: 				container:       &clabruntime.GenericContainer{Names: []string{"n1"}},
318: 				includeSnapshot: tt.includeSnapshot,
319: 			}
320: 
321: 			states := map[int]ifaceSnapshot{}
322: 			if tt.known != nil {
323: 				states[7] = snapshotFromLink(tt.known)
324: 			}
325: 
326: 			var vanished map[int]struct{}
327: 			if tt.vanished {
328: 				vanished = map[int]struct{}{7: {}}
329: 			}
330: 
331: 			watcher.processExisting(states, nil, link(1500), nil, vanished, registry, tt.initial)
332: 			close(events)
333: 
334: 			var got []string
335: 			for ev := range events {
336: 				got = append(got, ev.Action)
337: 			}
338: 
339: 			if !slices.Equal(got, tt.want) {
340: 				t.Fatalf("actions = %v, want %v", got, tt.want)
341: 			}
342: 
343: 			if tt.vanished {
344: 				if _, ok := states[7]; ok {
345: 					t.Fatal("vanished link is tracked again")
346: 				}
347: 
348: 				return
349: 			}
350: 
351: 			if states[7].MTU != 1500 {
352: 				t.Fatalf("state MTU = %d, want 1500", states[7].MTU)
353: 			}
354: 		})
355: 	}
356: }

358: // TestProcessUpdateDeleteOnlyTrackedLinks checks that a deletion is reported
359: // once, also when its notification arrives after a resubscribe has already
360: // reported it.
361: func TestProcessUpdateDeleteOnlyTrackedLinks(t *testing.T) {
362: 	link := &netlink.Dummy{LinkAttrs: netlink.LinkAttrs{Index: 7, Name: "eth1", MTU: 1500}}
363: 	update := netlink.LinkUpdate{Link: link}
364: 	update.Header.Type = unix.RTM_DELLINK

367: 	registry := newNetlinkRegistry(t.Context(), events, false, false, 0)

369: 		container: &clabruntime.GenericContainer{Names: []string{"n1"}},
370: 	}

373:

375: 	watcher.processUpdate(states, nil, update, registry, nil)
376: 	close(events)
377:

379: 	for ev := range events {
380: 		got = append(got, ev.Action)
381: 	}
382:

384: 		t.Fatalf("actions = %v, want %v", got, want)
385: 	}
386: 
387: 	if _, ok := states[7]; ok {
388: 		t.Fatal("deleted link is still tracked")
389: 	}
390: }
391: 
392: // reportedEvent is the action and interface name of an emitted event.
393: type reportedEvent struct {
394: 	action string
395: 	ifname string
396: }
397: 
398: // drainEvents closes events and returns what was emitted, in order.
399: func drainEvents(events chan aggregatedEvent) []reportedEvent {
400: 	close(events)
401: 
402: 	var got []reportedEvent
403: 	for ev := range events {
404: 		got = append(got, reportedEvent{action: ev.Action, ifname: ev.Attributes["ifname"]})
405: 	}
406: 
407: 	return got
408: }

410: // stitchedWatcher returns a watcher that reported the link with index 7 as
411: // the host-side stitch interface of its veth-stitch link.
412: func stitchedWatcher() *netlinkWatcher {
413: 	return &netlinkWatcher{
414: 		container: &clabruntime.GenericContainer{Names: []string{"n1"}},
415: 		reported: map[int]reportedInterface{
416: 			7: {
417: 				attributes: map[string]string{"ifname": "clab-s-0011aabb", "alias": "e1-1"},
418: 				since:      time.Now(),
419: 			},
420: 		},
421: 	}
422: }

aggregatedEvent) *eventLog {\n\tl := &eventLog{actions: map[string][]string{}}\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\tcase ev := <-events:\n\t\t\t\tl.mu.Lock()\n\t\t\t\tname := 252: // Retry settings for a watcher that could not subscribe to link updates.
253: var (
254: 	watchMaxAttempts   = 5
255: 	watchRetryDelay    = time.Second
256: 	watchMaxRetryDelay = 10 * time.Second
