259: func (w *netlinkWatcher) run(ctx context.Context, registry *netlinkRegistry) {
260: 	defer close(w.done)
261: 	if w.container == nil {
262: 		return
263: 	}
264: 
265: 	defer registry.remove(w.container.ID, w)
266: 
267: 	containerName := firstContainerName(w.container)
268: 	if w.container.Runtime == nil {
269: 		log.Debugf("container %s has no runtime, skipping netlink watcher", containerName)
270: 
271: 		return
272: 	}
273: 
274: 	// The states outlive a subscription, so a resubscribe only reports what
275: 	// changed while no subscription was active.
276: 	states := make(map[int]ifaceSnapshot)
277: 
278: 	var statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample
279: 	if w.includeStats {
280: 		statsSamples = make(map[int]ifaceStatsSample)
281: 	}
282: 
283: 	initial := true
284: 	failures := 0
285: 	delay := watchRetryDelay
286: 
287: 	for {
288: 		subscribed, err := w.watch(ctx, registry, states, statsSamples, initial)
289: 		if ctx.Err() != nil {
290: 			return
291: 		}
292: 
293: 		if subscribed {
294: 			initial = false
295: 			failures = 0
296: 			delay = watchRetryDelay
297: 
298: 			log.Warnf(
299: 				"netlink subscription for container %s ended, resubscribing: %v",
300: 				containerName,
301: 				err,
302: 			)
303: 		} else {
304: 			failures++
305: 			if failures >= watchMaxAttempts {
306: 				log.Warnf("stopped watching interfaces of container %s: %v", containerName, err)
307: 
308: 				return
309: 			}
310: 
311: 			log.Debugf(
312: 				"failed to watch interfaces of container %s, retrying in %s: %v",
313: 				containerName,
314: 				delay,
315: 				err,
316: 			)
317: 		}
318: 
319: 		select {
320: 		case <-time.After(delay):
321: 		case <-ctx.Done():
322: 			return
323: 		}
324: 
325: 		delay = min(delay*2, watchMaxRetryDelay)
326: 	}
327: }
328: 
329: // watch subscribes to link updates in the container's namespace and handles
330: // them until ctx ends or the subscription fails. It reports whether it
331: // subscribed. The subscription lists the existing links itself, so no change
332: // between listing and subscribing is lost.
333: func (w *netlinkWatcher) watch(
334: 	ctx context.Context,
335: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
336: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
337: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
338: 	initial bool,
339: ) (bool, error) {
340: 	nsPath, err := waitForNamespacePath(ctx, w.container.Runtime, w.container.ID)
341: 	if err != nil {
342: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to resolve netns: %w", err)
343: 	}
344: 
345: 	nsHandle, err := netns.GetFromPath(nsPath)
346: 	if err != nil {
347: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to open netns: %w", err)
348: 	}
349: 	defer nsHandle.Close()
350: 
351: 	w.nsHandle = nsHandle
352: 
353: 	netHandle, err := netlink.NewHandleAt(nsHandle)
354: 	if err != nil {
355: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create netlink handle: %w", err)
356: 	}
357: 	defer netHandle.Close()
358: 
359: 	netemQuery := newNetemQuerier(nsHandle)
360: 	defer netemQuery.Close()
361: 
362: 	netemInfos := netemQuery.Query()
363: 
364: 	updates := make(chan netlink.LinkUpdate, 32)
365: 	done := make(chan struct{})
366: 	defer close(done)
367: 
368: 	var (
369: 		errMu     sync.Mutex
370: 		lastError error
371: 	)
372: 
373: 	opts := netlink.LinkSubscribeOptions{
374: 		Namespace:    &nsHandle,
375: 		ListExisting: true,
376: 		// An interrupted listing is reported here too. It needs no action:
377: 		// links that changed during the listing also arrive as updates.
378: 		ErrorCallback: func(err error) {
379: 			log.Debugf(
380: 				"netlink subscription for container %s: %v",
381: 				firstContainerName(w.container),
382: 				err,
383: 			)
384: 
385: 			errMu.Lock()
386: 			lastError = err
387: 			errMu.Unlock()
388: 		},
389: 	}
390: 
391: 	if err := netlink.LinkSubscribeWithOptions(updates, done, opts); err != nil {
392: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to subscribe to netlink updates: %w", err)
393: 	}
394: 
395: 	var vanished map[int]struct{}
396: 	if len(states) > 0 {
397: 		vanished = w.removeVanishedLinks(netHandle, states, statsSamples, registry)
398: 	}
399: 
400: 	// Set up netem polling interval (reuse stats interval or default to 1s).
401: 	netemPollInterval := w.statsInterval
402: 	if netemPollInterval == 0 {
403: 		netemPollInterval = time.Second
404: 	}
405: 	netemTicker := time.NewTicker(netemPollInterval)
406: 	defer netemTicker.Stop()
407: 
408: 	var (
409: 		ticker  *time.Ticker
410: 		tickerC <-chan time.Time
411: 	)
412: 	if w.includeStats && w.statsInterval > 0 {
413: 		ticker = time.NewTicker(w.statsInterval)
414: 		tickerC = ticker.C
415: 		defer ticker.Stop()
416: 	}
417: 
418: 	for {
419: 		select {
420: 		case <-tickerC:
421: 			w.collectAndEmitStats(netHandle, states, statsSamples, registry)
422: 		case <-netemTicker.C:
423: 			w.pollNetemChanges(states, registry, netemQuery)
424: 		case <-ctx.Done():
425: 			return true, nil
426: 		case update, ok := <-updates:
427: 			if !ok {
428: 				errMu.Lock()
429: 				err := lastError
430: 				errMu.Unlock()
431: 
432: 				if err == nil {
433: 					err = errors.New("update channel closed")
434: 				}
435: 
436: 				return true, err
437: 			}
438: 
439: 			// Replies to the listing carry NLM_F_MULTI, notifications do not.
440: 			if update.Header.Flags&unix.NLM_F_MULTI != 0 {
441: 				w.processExisting(
442: 					states,
443: 					statsSamples,
444: 					update.Link,
445: 					netemInfos,
446: 					vanished,
447: 					registry,
448: 					initial,
449: 				)
450: 
451: 				continue
452: 			}
453: 
454: 			w.processUpdate(states, statsSamples, update, registry, netemQuery)
455: 		}
456: 	}
457: }
458: 
459: // processExisting records a link listed when the subscription started. On the
460: // first subscription a new link is reported as a snapshot when requested; after
461: // a resubscribe it is reported as created.
462: //
463: // The listing can be older than the check in removeVanishedLinks, so it can
464: // still hold a link that check reported as deleted. Such a link is skipped; its
465: // deletion notification follows and is ignored, as the link is not tracked.
466: func (w *netlinkWatcher) processExisting(
467: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
468: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
469: 	link netlink.Link,
470: 	netemInfos map[int]netemInfo,
471: 	vanished map[int]struct{},
472: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
473: 	initial bool,
474: ) {
475: 	if link == nil || link.Attrs() == nil {
476: 		return
477: 	}
478: 
479: 	if _, ok := vanished[link.Attrs().Index]; ok {
480: 		return
481: 	}
482: 
483: 	snapshot := withNetem(snapshotFromLink(link), netemInfos)
484: 	previous, exists := states[snapshot.Index]
485: 
486: 	if exists && snapshot.equal(previous) {
487: 		return
488: 	}
489: 
490: 	states[snapshot.Index] = snapshot
491: 	if statsSamples != nil {
492: 		if sample, ok := newStatsSample(snapshot, time.Now()); ok {
493: 			statsSamples[snapshot.Index] = sample
494: 		}
495: 	}
496: 
497: 	switch {
498: 	case exists:
499: 		registry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, "update", snapshot)
500: 	case !initial:
501: 		registry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, "create", snapshot)
502: 	case w.includeSnapshot:
503: 		registry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, "snapshot", snapshot)
504: 	}
505: }
506: 
507: // removeVanishedLinks reports the links that were deleted while no
508: // subscription was active and returns their indexes.
509: func (w *netlinkWatcher) removeVanishedLinks(
510: 	netHandle *netlink.Handle,
511: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
512: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
513: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
514: ) map[int]struct{} {
515: 	current, err := snapshotInterfaces(netHandle)
516: 	if err != nil {
517: 		log.Debugf(
518: 			"failed to list interfaces of container %s: %v",
519: 			firstContainerName(w.container),
520: 			err,
521: 		)
522: 
523: 		return nil
524: 	}
525: 
526: 	vanished := map[int]struct{}{}
527: 
528: 	for idx, snapshot := range states {
529: 		if _, ok := current[idx]; ok {
530: 			continue
531: 		}
532: 
533: 		vanished[idx] = struct{}{}
534: 
535: 		delete(states, idx)
536: 		delete(statsSamples, idx)
537: 		registry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, "delete", snapshot)
538: 	}
539: 
540: 	return vanished
541: }
542: 
543: func (w *netlinkWatcher) processUpdate(
544: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
545: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
546: 	update netlink.LinkUpdate,
547: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
548: 	netemQuery *netemQuerier,
549: ) {
550: 	if update.Link == nil {
551: 		return
552: 	}
553: 
554: 	attrs := update.Link.Attrs()
555: 	if attrs == nil {
556: 		return
557: 	}
558: 
559: 	snapshot := snapshotFromLink(update.Link)
560: 	previous, exists := states[snapshot.Index]
561: 
562: 	switch update.Header.Type {
563: 	case unix.RTM_DELLINK:
564: 		// A link that is not tracked was never reported, or its deletion was
565: 		// already reported when the links were listed again after a resubscribe.
566: 		if !exists {
567: 			return
568: 		}
569: 
570: 		delete(states, snapshot.Index)
571: 		delete(statsSamples, snapshot.Index)
572: 		registry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, "delete", previous)
573: 	case unix.RTM_NEWLINK:
574: 		// Query netem info for this interface
575: 		snapshot = withNetem(snapshot, netemQuery.Query())
576: 
577: 		if exists && snapshot.equal(previous) {
578: 			return
579: 		}
580: 
581: 		action := "create"
582: 		if exists {
583: 			action = "update"
584: 		}
585: 
586: 		states[snapshot.Index] = snapshot
587: 		if w.includeStats && statsSamples != nil {
588: 			if sample, ok := newStatsSample(snapshot, time.Now()); ok {
589: 				statsSamples[snapshot.Index] = sample
590: 			}
591: 		}
592: 		registry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, action, snapshot)
593: 	}
594: }
595: 
596: // pollNetemChanges checks for netem changes and emits update events.
597: func (w *netlinkWatcher) pollNetemChanges(
598: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
599: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
600: 	netemQuery *netemQuerier,
601: ) {
602: 	netemInfos := netemQuery.Query()
603: 
604: 	for idx, snapshot := range states {
605: 		previous := snapshot
606: 		info, hasNetem := netemInfos[idx]
607: 
608: 		if hasNetem {
609: 			snapshot.HasNetem = true
610: 			snapshot.Delay = info.Delay
611: 			snapshot.Jitter = info.Jitter
612: 			snapshot.PacketLoss = info.PacketLoss
613: 			snapshot.Rate = info.Rate
614: 			snapshot.Corruption = info.Corruption
615: 		} else if snapshot.HasNetem {
616: 			// Netem was removed
617: 			snapshot.HasNetem = false
618: 			snapshot.Delay = ""
619: 			snapshot.Jitter = ""
620: 			snapshot.PacketLoss = 0
621: 			snapshot.Rate = 0
622: 			snapshot.Corruption = 0
623: 		}
624: 
625: 		if !snapshot.equal(previous) {
626: 			states[idx] = snapshot
627: 			registry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, "update", snapshot)
628: 		}
629: 	}

The subscription lists the existing links itself, so no change\n// between listing and subscribing is lost.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) watch(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n\tinitial bool,\n) (bool, error) {\n\tnsPath, err := waitForNamespacePath(ctx, w.container.Runtime, w.container.ID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to resolve netns: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnsHandle, err := netns.GetFromPath(nsPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open netns: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer nsHandle.Close()\n\n\tw.nsHandle = nsHandle\n\n\tnetHandle, err := netlink.NewHandleAt(nsHandle)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create netlink handle: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer netHandle.Close()\n\n\tnetemQuery := newNetemQuerier(nsHandle)\n\tdefer netemQuery.Close()\n\n\tnetemInfos := netemQuery.Query()\n\n\tupdates := make(chan netlink.LinkUpdate, 32)\n\tdone := make(chan struct{})\n\tdefer close(done)\n\n\tvar (\n\t\terrMu     sync.Mutex\n\t\tlastError error\n\t)\n\n\topts := netlink.LinkSubscribeOptions{\n\t\tNamespace:    &nsHandle,\n\t\tListExisting: true,\n\t\t// An interrupted listing is reported here too. It needs no action:\n\t\t// links that changed during the listing also arrive as updates.\n\t\tErrorCallback: func(err error) {\n\t\t\tlog.Debugf(\n\t\t\t\t\"netlink subscription for container %s: %v\",\n\t\t\t\tfirstContainerName(w.container),\n\t\t\t\terr,\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\terrMu.Lock()\n\t\t\tlastError = err\n\t\t\terrMu.Unlock()\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tif err := netlink.LinkSubscribeWithOptions(updates, done, opts); On the\n// first subscription a new link is reported as a snapshot when requested; after\n// a resubscribe it is reported as created.\n//\n// The listing can be older than the check in removeVanishedLinks, so it can\n// still hold a link that check reported as deleted. Such a link is skipped; its\n// deletion notification follows and is ignored, as the link is not tracked.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) processExisting(\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n\tlink netlink.Link,\n\tnetemInfos map[int]netemInfo,\n\tvanished map[int]struct{},\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tinitial bool,\n) {\n\tif link == nil || link.Attrs() == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif _, ok := vanished[link.Attrs().Index]; ok {\n\t\t\tstatsSamples[snapshot.Index] = sample\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tswitch {\n\tcase exists:\n\t\tregistry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"update\", snapshot)\n\tcase !initial:\n\t\tregistry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"create\", snapshot)\n\tcase w.includeSnapshot:\n\t\tregistry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"snapshot\", snapshot)\n\t}\n}\n\n// removeVanishedLinks reports the links that were deleted while no\n// subscription was active and returns their indexes.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) removeVanishedLinks(\n\tnetHandle *netlink.Handle,\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n) map[int]struct{} {\n\tcurrent, err := snapshotInterfaces(netHandle)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Debugf(\n\t\t\t\"failed to list interfaces of container %s: %v\",\n\t\t\tfirstContainerName(w.container),\n\t\t\terr,\n\t\t)\n\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tvanished := map[int]struct{}{}\n\n\tfor idx, snapshot := range states {\n\t\tif _, ok := current[idx]; ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvanished[idx] = struct{}{}\n\n\t\tdelete(states, idx)\n\t\tdelete(statsSamples, idx)\n\t\tregistry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"delete\", snapshot)\n\t}\n\n\treturn vanished\n}\n\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) processUpdate(\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tstatsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,\n\tupdate netlink.LinkUpdate,\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tnetemQuery *netemQuerier,\n) {\n\tif update.Link == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tattrs := update.Link.Attrs()\n\tif attrs == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsnapshot := snapshotFromLink(update.Link)\n\tprevious, exists := states[snapshot.Index]\n\n\tswitch update.Header.Type {\n\tcase unix.RTM_DELLINK:\n\t\t// A link that is not tracked was never reported, or its deletion was\n\t\t// already reported when the links were listed again after a resubscribe.\n\t\tif !exists {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tdelete(states, snapshot.Index)\n\t\tdelete(statsSamples, snapshot.Index)\n\t\tregistry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"delete\", previous)\n\tcase unix.RTM_NEWLINK:\n\t\t// Query netem info for this interface\n\t\tsnapshot = withNetem(snapshot, netemQuery.Query())\n\n\t\tif exists && snapshot.equal(previous) {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\taction := \"create\"\n\t\tif exists {\n\t\t\taction = \"update\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tstates[snapshot.Index] = snapshot\n\t\tif w.includeStats && statsSamples != nil {\n\t\t\tif sample, ok := newStatsSample(snapshot, time.Now()); ok {\n\t\t\t\tstatsSamples[snapshot.Index] = sample\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tregistry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, action, snapshot)\n\t}\n}\n\n// pollNetemChanges checks for netem changes and emits update events.\nfunc (w *netlinkWatcher) pollNetemChanges(\n\tstates map[int]ifaceSnapshot,\n\tregistry *netlinkRegistry,\n\tnetemQuery *netemQuerier,\n) {\n\tnetemInfos := netemQuery.Query()\n\n\tfor idx, snapshot := range states {\n\t\tprevious := snapshot\n\t\tinfo, hasNetem := netemInfos[idx]\n\n\t\tif hasNetem {\n\t\t\tsnapshot.HasNetem = true\n\t\t\tsnapshot.Delay = info.Delay\n\t\t\tsnapshot.Jitter = info.Jitter\n\t\t\tsnapshot.PacketLoss = info.PacketLoss\n\t\t\tsnapshot.Rate = info.Rate\n\t\t\tsnapshot.Corruption = info.Corruption\n\t\t} else if snapshot.HasNetem {\n\t\t\t// Netem was removed\n\t\t\tsnapshot.HasNetem = false\n\t\t\tsnapshot.Delay = \"\"\n\t\t\tsnapshot.Jitter = \"\"\n\t\t\tsnapshot.PacketLoss = 0\n\t\t\tsnapshot.Rate = 0\n\t\t\tsnapshot.Corruption = 0\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif !snapshot.equal(previous) {\n\t\t\tstates[idx] = snapshot\n\t\t\tregistry.emitInterfaceEvent(w.container, \"update\", snapshot)\n\t\t}\n\t}",

839: func waitForNamespacePath(
840: 	ctx context.Context,
841: 	runtime clabruntime.ContainerRuntime,
842: 	containerID string,
843: ) (string, error) {
844: 	const (
845: 		attempts   = 5
846: 		retryDelay = 200 * time.Millisecond
847: 	)
848: 
849: 	var lastErr error
850: 
851: 	for i := 0; i < attempts; i++ {
852: 		nsPath, err := runtime.GetNSPath(ctx, containerID)
853: 		if err == nil && nsPath != "" {
854: 			return nsPath, nil
855: 		}
856: 
857: 		if err != nil {
858: 			lastErr = err
859: 		}
860: 
861: 		select {
862: 		case <-time.After(retryDelay):
863: 		case <-ctx.Done():
864: 			return "", ctx.Err()
865: 		}
866: 	}
867: 
868: 	if lastErr != nil {
869: 		return "", lastErr
870: 	}
871: 
872: 	return "", fmt.Errorf("namespace path not ready for container %s", containerID)
873: }

903: func snapshotInterfaces(netHandle *netlink.Handle) (map[int]ifaceSnapshot, error) {
904: 	if netHandle == nil {
905: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("netlink handle is nil")
906: 	}
907: 
908: 	links, err := netHandle.LinkList()
909: 	if err != nil {
910: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unable to list links: %w", err)
911: 	}
912: 
913: 	states := make(map[int]ifaceSnapshot, len(links))
914: 	for _, link := range links {
915: 		snapshot := snapshotFromLink(link)
916: 		states[snapshot.Index] = snapshot
917: 	}
918: 
919: 	return states, nil
920: }

922: // withNetem returns the snapshot with the netem settings of its interface.
923: func withNetem(snapshot ifaceSnapshot, netemInfos map[int]netemInfo) ifaceSnapshot {
924: 	if info, ok := netemInfos[snapshot.Index]; ok {
925: 		snapshot.HasNetem = true

931: 	}

936: func snapshotFromLink(link netlink.Link) ifaceSnapshot {

1023: func newStatsSample(snapshot ifaceSnapshot, timestamp time.Time) (ifaceStatsSample, bool) {
1024: 	if !snapshot.HasStats {
1025: 		return ifaceStatsSample{}, false
1026: 	}
1027: 
1028: 	return ifaceStatsSample{
1029: 		RxBytes:   snapshot.RxBytes,
1030: 		TxBytes:   snapshot.TxBytes,
1031: 		RxPackets: snapshot.RxPackets,
1032: 		TxPackets: snapshot.TxPackets,
1033: 		Timestamp: timestamp,
1034: 	}, true
1035: }
1036: 
1037: func (w *netlinkWatcher) collectAndEmitStats(
1038: 	netHandle *netlink.Handle,
1039: 	states map[int]ifaceSnapshot,
1040: 	statsSamples map[int]ifaceStatsSample,
1041: 	registry *netlinkRegistry,
1042: ) {
1043: 	if netHandle == nil || statsSamples == nil {
1044: 		return
1045: 	}
1046: 
1047: 	now := time.Now()
1048: 	// Fetch all interfaces with one netlink call per tick instead of one call
1049: 	// per interface to reduce syscall overhead in large labs.
1050: 	currentStates, err := snapshotInterfaces(netHandle)
1051: 	if err != nil {
1052: 		log.Debugf(
1053: 			"failed to collect interface stats snapshot for container %s: %v",
1054: 			firstContainerName(w.container),
1055: 			err,
1056: 		)
1057: 
1058: 		return
1059: 	}
1060: 
1061: 	for idx, state := range states {
1062: 		current, ok := currentStates[idx]
1063: 		if !ok {
1064: 			continue
1065: 		}
1066: 
1067: 		state.Name = current.Name
1068: 		state.Alias = current.Alias
1069: 		state.MTU = current.MTU
1070: 		state.MAC = current.MAC
1071: 		state.OperState = current.OperState
1072: 		state.Type = current.Type
1073: 		state.HasStats = current.HasStats
1074: 		state.RxBytes = current.RxBytes
1075: 		state.TxBytes = current.TxBytes
1076: 		state.RxPackets = current.RxPackets
1077: 		state.TxPackets = current.TxPackets
1078: 		states[idx] = state
1079: 
1080: 		if !state.HasStats {
1081: 			delete(statsSamples, idx)
1082: 
1083: 			continue
1084: 		}
1085: 
1086: 		prev, ok := statsSamples[idx]
1087: 		if !ok || now.Sub(prev.Timestamp) <= 0 {
1088: 			statsSamples[idx] = ifaceStatsSample{
1089: 				RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1090: 				TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1091: 				RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1092: 				TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1093: 				Timestamp: now,
1094: 			}
1095: 
1096: 			continue
1097: 		}
1098: 
1099: 		interval := now.Sub(prev.Timestamp)
1100: 		if interval <= 0 {
1101: 			statsSamples[idx] = ifaceStatsSample{
1102: 				RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1103: 				TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1104: 				RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1105: 				TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1106: 				Timestamp: now,
1107: 			}
1108: 
1109: 			continue
1110: 		}
1111: 
1112: 		rxBytesDelta := deltaCounter(prev.RxBytes, state.RxBytes)
1113: 		txBytesDelta := deltaCounter(prev.TxBytes, state.TxBytes)
1114: 		rxPacketsDelta := deltaCounter(prev.RxPackets, state.RxPackets)
1115: 		txPacketsDelta := deltaCounter(prev.TxPackets, state.TxPackets)
1116: 
1117: 		seconds := interval.Seconds()
1118: 		if seconds <= 0 {
1119: 			statsSamples[idx] = ifaceStatsSample{
1120: 				RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1121: 				TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1122: 				RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1123: 				TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1124: 				Timestamp: now,
1125: 			}
1126: 
1127: 			continue
1128: 		}
1129: 
1130: 		metrics := ifaceStatsMetrics{
1131: 			RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1132: 			TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1133: 			RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1134: 			TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1135: 			RxBps:     float64(rxBytesDelta) * 8 / seconds,
1136: 			TxBps:     float64(txBytesDelta) * 8 / seconds,
1137: 			RxPps:     float64(rxPacketsDelta) / seconds,
1138: 			TxPps:     float64(txPacketsDelta) / seconds,
1139: 			Interval:  interval,
1140: 			Timestamp: now,
1141: 		}
1142: 
1143: 		registry.emitInterfaceStatsEvent(w.container, state, metrics)
1144: 
1145: 		statsSamples[idx] = ifaceStatsSample{
1146: 			RxBytes:   state.RxBytes,
1147: 			TxBytes:   state.TxBytes,
1148: 			RxPackets: state.RxPackets,
1149: 			TxPackets: state.TxPackets,
1150: 			Timestamp: now,
1151: 		}
1152: 	}
1153: }
1154: 
1155: func deltaCounter(previous, current uint64) uint64 {
1156: 	if current >= previous {
1157: 		return current - previous
1158: 	}
1159: 
1160: 	return current
1161: }

1184: 	q.tcHandle = nil

1186: 
1187: // Query queries the TC qdiscs in the namespace and returns a map of
1188: // interface index to netem data.
1189: func (q *netemQuerier) Query() map[int]netemInfo {
1190: 	result := make(map[int]netemInfo)
1191: 
1192: 	qdiscs, err := q.qdiscs()
1193: 	if err != nil {
1194: 		log.Debugf("failed to query tc qdiscs: %v", err)
1195: 		return result
1196: 	}
1197: 
1198: 	for idx := range qdiscs {
1199: 		qdisc := &qdiscs[idx]
1200: 		if qdisc.Attribute.Kind != "netem" || qdisc.Netem == nil {
1201: 			continue
1202: 		}
1203: 
1204: 		info := netemInfoFromQdisc(qdisc)
1205: 		if info.hasValues() {
1206: 			result[int(qdisc.Ifindex)] = info
1207: 		}
1208: 	}
1209: 
1210: 	return result
1211: }
1212: 
1213: func (q *netemQuerier) qdiscs() ([]gotc.Object, error) {
1214: 	if q == nil {
1215: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("netem querier is nil")
1216: 	}
1217: 
1218: 	if err := q.ensureHandle(); err != nil {
1219: 		return nil, err
1220: 	}
1221: 
1222: 	qdiscs, err := clabnetem.Impairments(q.tcHandle)
1223: 	if err == nil {
1224: 		return qdiscs, nil
1225: 	}
1226: 
1227: 	// Retry once by reopening the tc socket in case it became stale.
1228: 	q.Close()
1229: 	if errOpen := q.ensureHandle(); errOpen != nil {
1230: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
1231: 			"failed to query tc qdiscs (%v), and failed to reopen tc socket (%v)",
1232: 			err,
1233: 			errOpen,
1234: 		)
1235: 	}
1236: 
1237: 	return clabnetem.Impairments(q.tcHandle)
1238: }
1239: 
1240: func (q *netemQuerier) ensureHandle() error {
1241: 	if q == nil {
1242: 		return fmt.Errorf("netem querier is nil")
1243: 	}
1244: 
1245: 	if q.tcHandle != nil {
1246: 		return nil
1247: 	}
1248: 
1249: 	tcnl, err := clabnetem.NewTC(int(q.nsHandle))
1250: 	if err != nil {
1251: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open tc socket for netem query: %w", err)
1252: 	}
1253: 
1254: 	q.tcHandle = tcnl
1255: 
1256: 	return nil
1257: }
1258: 
1259: type netemInfo struct {
1260: 	Delay      string
1261: 	Jitter     string
1262: 	PacketLoss float64
1263: 	Rate       int
1264: 	Corruption float64
1265: }
1266: 
1267: func (n netemInfo) hasValues() bool {
1268: 	return n.Delay != "" || n.Jitter != "" || n.PacketLoss != 0 || n.Rate != 0 || n.Corruption != 0
1269: }
1270: 
1271: func netemInfoFromQdisc(qdisc *gotc.Object) netemInfo {
1272: 	var info netemInfo
1273: 
1274: 	if qdisc.Netem == nil {
1275: 		return info
1276: 	}
1277: 
1278: 	if qdisc.Netem.Latency64 != nil && *qdisc.Netem.Latency64 != 0 {
1279: 		info.Delay = (time.Duration(*qdisc.Netem.Latency64) * time.Nanosecond).String()
1280: 	}
1281: 
1282: 	if qdisc.Netem.Jitter64 != nil && *qdisc.Netem.Jitter64 != 0 {
1283: 		info.Jitter = (time.Duration(*qdisc.Netem.Jitter64) * time.Nanosecond).String()
1284: 	}
1285: 
1286: 	if qdisc.Netem.Rate != nil && qdisc.Netem.Rate.Rate != 0 {
1287: 		info.Rate = int(qdisc.Netem.Rate.Rate * 8 / msPerSec)
1288: 	}
1289: 
1290: 	if qdisc.Netem.Corrupt != nil && qdisc.Netem.Corrupt.Probability != 0 {
1291: 		info.Corruption = math.Round((float64(qdisc.Netem.Corrupt.Probability)/
1292: 			float64(math.MaxUint32)*100)*100) / 100
1293: 	}
1294: 
1295: 	if qdisc.Netem.Qopt.Loss != 0 {
1296: 		info.PacketLoss = math.Round(
1297: 			(float64(qdisc.Netem.Qopt.Loss)/float64(math.MaxUint32)*100)*100) / 100
1298: 	}
1299: 
1300: 	return info
1301: }

36: 		return nil, nil
37: 	}
38:

40: 		return nil, err
41: 	}

71: // LinkVEthStitched is the resolved veth-stitch link: two veth segments
72: // (nodeA<->root-ns, nodeB<->root-ns) whose root-ns ends are joined by a tc stitch.
73: type LinkVEthStitched struct {
74: 	LinkCommonParams
75: 
76: 	lifecycleMutex sync.Mutex
77: 
78: 	// since the stitch veth lives in the root ns, we need to ensure altnames
79: 	// are per-lab unique.
80: 	labName string
81: 
82: 	segA *LinkVEth
83: 	segB *LinkVEth
84: 
85: 	// the two root-ns endpoints that get stitched together
86: 	epA Endpoint
87: 	epB Endpoint
88: }
89: 
90: func (*LinkVEthStitched) GetType() LinkType {
91: 	return LinkTypeVethStitch
92: }
93: 
94: func (l *LinkVEthStitched) GetEndpoints() []Endpoint {
95: 	return []Endpoint{l.segA.Endpoints[0], l.segB.Endpoints[0]}
96: }
97: 
98: // GetRuntimeEndpoints returns every material endpoint managed by the stitched link.
99: func (l *LinkVEthStitched) GetRuntimeEndpoints() []Endpoint {
100: 	return []Endpoint{l.segA.Endpoints[0], l.segB.Endpoints[0], l.epA, l.epB}
101: }
102: 
103: // Deploy creates the veth segment belonging to ep.
104: func (l *LinkVEthStitched) Deploy(ctx context.Context, ep Endpoint) error {
105: 	l.lifecycleMutex.Lock()
106: 	defer l.lifecycleMutex.Unlock()
107: 
108: 	if l.DeploymentState == LinkDeploymentStateFullDeployed {
109: 		return nil
110: 	}
111: 
112: 	var err error
113: 	switch ep {
114: 	case l.segA.Endpoints[0]:
115: 		err = l.segA.Deploy(ctx, ep)
116: 	case l.segB.Endpoints[0]:
117: 		err = l.segB.Deploy(ctx, ep)
118: 	default:
119: 		return fmt.Errorf("endpoint %s does not belong to link [ %s, %s ]",
120: 			ep, l.segA.Endpoints[0], l.segB.Endpoints[0])
121: 	}
122: 	if err != nil {
123: 		return err
124: 	}
125: 
126: 	l.DeploymentState = LinkDeploymentStateHalfDeployed
127: 
128: 	if l.segA.DeploymentState == LinkDeploymentStateFullDeployed &&
129: 		l.segB.DeploymentState == LinkDeploymentStateFullDeployed {
130: 		return l.stitchSegments(ctx)
131: 	}
132: 
133: 	return nil
134: }
135: 
136: // PostDeploy tags the two root-ns far ends and joins them with tc.
137: // if they weren't already stitched in Deploy.
138: func (l *LinkVEthStitched) PostDeploy(ctx context.Context) error {
139: 	l.lifecycleMutex.Lock()
140: 	defer l.lifecycleMutex.Unlock()
141: 
142: 	if l.DeploymentState == LinkDeploymentStateFullDeployed {
143: 		return nil
144: 	}
145: 	if l.segA.DeploymentState != LinkDeploymentStateFullDeployed ||
146: 		l.segB.DeploymentState != LinkDeploymentStateFullDeployed {
147: 		return fmt.Errorf("cannot stitch veth segments before both are fully deployed")
148: 	}
149: 
150: 	return l.stitchSegments(ctx)
151: }
152: 
153: func (l *LinkVEthStitched) stitchSegments(ctx context.Context) error {
154: 	steps := []func() error{
155: 		func() error { return markFarEnd(l.epB, l.segA.Endpoints[0], l.labName) },
156: 		func() error { return markFarEnd(l.epA, l.segB.Endpoints[0], l.labName) },
157: 		func() error { return stitch(l.epA, l.epB) },
158: 		func() error { return stitch(l.epB, l.epA) },
159: 	}
160: 	for _, step := range steps {
161: 		if err := retryTransientNetlink(ctx, "veth-stitch", step); err != nil {
162: 			return err
163: 		}
164: 	}
165: 
166: 	l.DeploymentState = LinkDeploymentStateFullDeployed
167: 
168: 	return nil
169: }
170: 
171: func (l *LinkVEthStitched) Remove(ctx context.Context) error {
172: 	l.lifecycleMutex.Lock()
173: 	defer l.lifecycleMutex.Unlock()
174: 
175: 	if l.DeploymentState == LinkDeploymentStateRemoved {
176: 		return nil
177: 	}
178: 
179: 	err := errors.Join(l.segA.Remove(ctx), l.segB.Remove(ctx))
180: 
181: 	l.DeploymentState = LinkDeploymentStateRemoved
182: 
183: 	return err
184: }
185: 
186: // buildVEthStitchSegment builds one veth segment: the real node endpoint plus a
187: // root-namespace far end named with a hash.
188: func buildVEthStitchSegment(
189: 	params *ResolveParams,
190: 	owner *LinkVEthStitched,
191: 	nodeEpRaw *EndpointRaw,
192: 	mtu int,
193: ) (*LinkVEth, Endpoint, error) {
194: 	seg := NewLinkVEth()
195: 	seg.MTU = mtu
196: 
197: 	nodeEp, err := nodeEpRaw.Resolve(params, owner)
198: 	if err != nil {
199: 		return nil, nil, err
200: 	}
201: 
202: 	// the far end sits in the root namespace (host node) with a short hash name
203: 	// that fits the 15 char name limit.
204: 	farName := stitchFarEndName(params.LabName, nodeEpRaw.Node, nodeEpRaw.Iface)
205: 	farGeneric := NewEndpointGeneric(GetHostLinkNode(), farName, seg)
206: 	farGeneric.MAC = nodeEp.GetMac()
207: 	farEp := NewEndpointHost(farGeneric)
208: 	seg.Endpoints = []Endpoint{nodeEp, farEp}
209: 
210: 	return seg, farEp, nil
211: }
212: 
213: func stitchFarEndName(labName, node, iface string) string {
214: 	sum := sha1.Sum([]byte(labName + "\x00" + node + "\x00" + iface))
215: 	return "clab-s-" + hex.EncodeToString(sum[:4]) // 7 + 8 = 15 chars
216: }
217: 
218: func markFarEnd(farEp, nodeEp Endpoint, labName string) error {
219: 	l, err := netlink.LinkByName(farEp.GetIfaceName())
220: 	if err != nil {
221: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to look up veth-stitch far end %q: %w", farEp.GetIfaceName(), err)
222: 	}
223: 
224: 	iface := nodeEp.GetIfaceAlias()
225: 	if iface == "" {
226: 		iface = nodeEp.GetIfaceName()
227: 	}
228: 
229: 	altName := StitchAltName(labName, nodeEp.GetNode().GetShortName(), iface)
230: 	if err := netlink.LinkAddAltName(l, altName); err != nil && !isAltNameNotSupportedErr(err) {
231: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to add veth-stitch altname %q: %w", altName, err)
232: 	}
233: 
234: 	return nil
235: }
236: 
237: func validateVEthStitched(r *LinkVEthStitchedRaw) error {
238: 	if len(r.Endpoints) != 2 {
239: 		return fmt.Errorf(
240: 			"veth-stitch links require exactly two endpoints, got %d",
241: 			len(r.Endpoints),
242: 		)
243: 	}
244: 
245: 	return nil
246: }
247: 
248: func (r *LinkVEthStitchedRaw) cleanupFilteredLink(
249: 	ctx context.Context,
250: 	labName string,
251: 	nodesFilter []string,
252: ) error {
253: 	return cleanupFilteredVethStitch(ctx, labName, nodesFilter, r.Endpoints)
254: }
255: 
256: func cleanupFilteredVethStitch(
257: 	ctx context.Context,
258: 	labName string,
259: 	nodesFilter []string,
260: 	endpoints []*EndpointRaw,
261: ) error {
262: 	for _, altName := range filteredVethStitchAltNames(labName, nodesFilter, endpoints) {
263: 		if err := RemoveOwnedInterface(ctx, GetHostLinkNode(), altName); err != nil {
264: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed cleaning up veth-stitch interface %q: %w", altName, err)
265: 		}
266: 	}
267: 
268: 	return nil
269: }
270: 
271: func filteredVethStitchAltNames(
272: 	labName string,
273: 	nodesFilter []string,
274: 	endpoints []*EndpointRaw,
275: ) []string {
276: 	names := make(map[string]struct{})
277: 	for _, ep := range endpoints {
278: 		if ep == nil || ep.Node == "" || ep.Iface == "" ||
279: 			!slices.Contains(nodesFilter, ep.Node) {
280: 			continue
281: 		}
282: 		names[StitchAltName(labName, ep.Node, ep.Iface)] = struct{}{}
283: 	}
284: 
285: 	result := make([]string, 0, len(names))
286: 	for name := range names {
287: 		result = append(result, name)
288: 	}
289: 	sort.Strings(result)
290: 
291: 	return result
292: }
293: 
294: // StitchAltName returns a unique name for veth-stitch links.
295: func StitchAltName(labName, node, iface string) string {
296: 	return "clab-stitch-" + clabutils.SanitizeInterfaceName(labName+"-"+node+"-"+iface)
297: }
298: 
299: // ToolsInterface returns the host-netns interface veth-stitch (and similar
300: // kinds) publish for node:iface under StitchAltName, or (nil, false) if none.
301: // Single seam for reporting/impairing the host side of the stitch.
302: func ToolsInterface(labName, node, iface string) (netlink.Link, bool) {
303: 	if labName == "" || node == "" || iface == "" {
304: 		return nil, false
305: 	}
306: 
307: 	link, err := netlink.LinkByName(StitchAltName(labName, node, iface))
308: 	if err != nil {
309: 		return nil, false
310: 	}
311: 
312: 	return link, true
313: }

(End of file - total 313 lines)

Reasoning
Now let me think about the changes:

1. New `reported` map on watcher: tracks what each link was last reported as (attributes + since).
2. `emit` replaces `emitInterfaceEvent` calls: tracks reported state, converts `update`→`create` when the link was reported under a different ifname before (after sending a delete under the old name).
3. `emitStats` moves stats emission to watcher; also emits a create event if the link was renamed (track returns true).
4. `reportStitchMarks`: on netem ticker tick, for veth links reported under their own name within stitchMarkWindow (1 minute), check whether the host-side stitch interface now exists (via `interfaceAttributes` which calls `overlayToolsInterface` → `ToolsInterface` → `netlink.LinkByName` in root namespace). If ifname differs (i.e., stitch interface now exists), emit an update (which will trigger track → delete old name → create new name... wait).

Wait — in `reportStitchMarks`, it calls `w.emit(registry, "update", snapshot)`. Inside emit, attributes = interfaceAttributes(...) which now resolves to stitch name. `track` sees previous ifname == snapshot.Name (the node's own name e.g. "eth1"), and new attributes ifname is "clab-s-..." — different → sends delete under previous name, returns true → action "update" → becomes "create". Then sends create with new attributes. Good.

Now, key issue: `emit` delete path. `w.reported[snapshot.Index]` attributes used as delete event attributes. But `attributes = previous.attributes` — this map is the stored clone. It's sent to the channel. Note `track` stores `maps.Clone(attributes)`, so the stored map is a private copy. OK.

Small but could be reordered. That's a minor perf/cleanliness point.

More important potential issues:

A. `reportStitchMarks` is only called on netemTicker ticks, and the netem poll interval defaults to 1s. If the stitch hasn't been marked yet, this returns not-ok, so each tick it retries. That's a netlink syscall per candidate link per tick for up to 1 minute (stitchMarkWindow). That's a deliberate design ("how long after a link is first reported its host-side stitch interface is looked for"). Cost: per-tick per-link LinkByName for up to a minute. Bounded by stitchMarkWindow. Probably acceptable; ponytail ceiling maybe. Could note as P4.

<path>/source/head/docs/cmd/events.md</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: # events command
2: 
3: ## Description
4: 
5: The `events` command streams lifecycle updates for every Containerlab resource and augments them with interface change notifications collected from the container network namespaces. The output combines the selected runtime's event feed (for example Docker) with the netlink information that powers `containerlab inspect interfaces`, so you can observe container activity and interface state changes in real time without selecting a specific lab.
6: 
7: ## Usage
8: 
9: `containerlab [global-flags] events [local-flags]`
10: 
11: **aliases:** `ev`
12: 
13: The command respects the global flags such as `--runtime`, `--debug`, or `--log-level`. It adds local options:
14: 
15: - `--format` controls the output representation (`plain`, `json`).
16: - `--initial-state` emits a snapshot of currently running containers and their interface states before following live updates.
17: - `--interface-stats` enables periodic interface counter sampling; leave unset to report only lifecycle and state changes.
18: - `--interface-stats-interval` customizes how frequently statistics are collected (for example `500ms`, `2s`, `1m`).
19: 
20: When invoked with no arguments it discovers all running labs and immediately begins streaming events; new labs that start after the command begins are picked up automatically.
21: 
22: ## Event format
23: 
24: In the default `plain` format every line mirrors the `docker events` format:
25: 
26: ```
27: <timestamp> <type> <action> <actor> (<key>=<value>, ...)
28: ```
29: 
30: - **Runtime events** show the short container ID as the actor and include the original attributes supplied by the container runtime (for example `image`, `name`, `containerlab`, `scope`, …). Container events are also enriched with `mgmt_ipv4`, `mgmt_ipv6`, and `ports` (published/exposed ports) when those values are available. When `--initial-state` is enabled the stream starts with `container <state>` snapshots (for example `container running`) that carry an `origin=snapshot` attribute.
31: - **Interface events** use type `interface` and `origin=netlink` in the attribute list. They also report interface-specific data such as `ifname`, `state`, `mtu`, `mac`, `type`, `alias`, and the lab label. The actor is still the container short ID, and the container name is supplied in the attributes (`name=...`).
32: - Interface notifications are emitted when a link appears, disappears, or when its relevant properties (operational state, MTU, alias, MAC address, type) change. Initial snapshots use the `snapshot` action when `--initial-state` is requested. When interface statistics are enabled the stream also includes `interface stats` updates with byte/packet counters and rate estimates.
33: 
34: When `--format json` is used, each event becomes a single JSON object on its own line. The fields match the plain output (`timestamp`, `type`, `action`, `actor_id`, `actor_name`, `actor_full_id`) and include an `attributes` map with the same key/value pairs that the plain formatter prints.
35: 
36: ## Examples
37: 
38: ### Watch an existing lab and new deployments
39: 
40: ```
41: $ containerlab events
42: 2024-07-01T11:02:56.123456000Z container start 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (containerlab=frr-lab, image=ghcr.io/srl-labs/frr, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, ports=0.0.0.0:2222/tcp->22;0.0.0.0:830/tcp->830)
43: 2024-07-01T11:02:57.004321000Z interface create 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (ifname=eth0, index=22, lab=frr-lab, mac=02:42:ac:14:00:02, mtu=1500, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=netlink, state=up, type=veth)
44: 2024-07-01T11:02:57.104512000Z interface update 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (ifname=eth0, index=22, lab=frr-lab, mac=02:42:ac:14:00:02, mtu=9000, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=netlink, state=up, type=veth)
45: 2024-07-01T11:05:12.918273000Z container die 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (containerlab=frr-lab, exitCode=0, image=ghcr.io/srl-labs/frr, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01)
46: 2024-07-01T11:05:13.018456000Z interface delete 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (ifname=eth0, index=22, lab=frr-lab, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=netlink, state=up, type=veth)
47: ```
48: 
49: The stream contains all currently running labs and stays active to capture subsequent deployments, restarts, or interface adjustments.
50: 
51: ### Include existing resources in the stream
52: 
53: ```
54: $ containerlab events --initial-state
55: 2024-07-01T11:02:55.912345000Z container running 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (containerlab=frr-lab, image=ghcr.io/srl-labs/frr, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=snapshot, ports=0.0.0.0:2222/tcp->22;0.0.0.0:830/tcp->830, state=running)
56: 2024-07-01T11:02:55.912678000Z interface snapshot 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (ifname=eth0, index=22, lab=frr-lab, mac=02:42:ac:14:00:02, mtu=1500, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=netlink, state=up, type=veth)
57: …
58: ```
59: 
60: This mode begins with a point-in-time view of every running container and interface before switching to live updates.
61: 
62: ### Include interface statistics
63: 
64: ```
65: containerlab events --interface-stats
66: ```
67: 
68: Statistics are disabled by default. Enabling them augments the feed with periodic counter samples in addition to lifecycle and state changes. Use `--interface-stats-interval` to balance fidelity with overhead: values between `1s` and `5s` work well for most labs, while larger deployments may prefer longer intervals (for example `10s`) to avoid excessive sampling load.
69: 
70: ### Use with alternative runtimes
71: 
72: Containerlab streams events from the runtime selected via the global `--runtime` flag.
73: 
74: Currently supported runtimes:
75: 
76: - `docker`
77: - `c9s`
78: 
79: With `--runtime c9s`, events are backed by kubernetes watches for Clabernetes
80: `Topology` and `Node` resources and labeled Pods. They use the actions and
81: attributes of the Docker runtime: a node is a container (`create`, `start`,
82: `kill`, `stop`, `die`, `destroy`, `health_status: …`) described by the same
83: labels, and the interface events (`snapshot`, `create`, `update`, `delete`,
84: `stats`) report the node's lab link interfaces, `eth0` and `lo` under the names
85: a Docker container of the node has. Interface state and statistics are sampled
86: inside the device containers, not through local host netlink. The stream covers
87: every namespace by default; `--namespace` (or `CLAB_KUBE_NAMESPACE`) limits it
88: to one namespace, and `-t`/`--name` to one lab. With Docker and Podman the lab
89: selection is ignored and every lab is streamed. The
90: [c9s runtime manual](../manual/clabernetes/runtime.md#events) lists the
91: attributes and the few Docker attributes c9s has no equivalent for.
92: 
93: Runtimes that do not implement the `events` API, or are not yet supported by Containerlab, will exit with an explanatory error.
94: 
95: ## See also
96: 
97: - [`inspect interfaces`](inspect/interfaces.md) – produces a point-in-time view of the same interface details that `events` reports continuously.
98: - [Clabernetes containerlab runtime](../manual/clabernetes/runtime.md#events) - describes c9s events and how they map onto Docker events.
99: - `docker events` – the raw runtime feed that Containerlab builds upon.

(End of file - total 99 lines)
</content>

The output combines the selected runtime's event feed (for example Docker) with the netlink information that powers `containerlab inspect interfaces`, so you can observe container activity and interface state changes in real time without selecting a specific lab.\n\n## Usage\n\n`containerlab [global-flags] events [local-flags]`\n\n**aliases:** `ev`\n\nThe command respects the global flags such as `--runtime`, `--debug`, or `--log-level`. It adds local options:\n\n- `--format` controls the output representation (`plain`, `json`).\n- `--initial-state` emits a snapshot of currently running containers and their interface states before following live updates.\n- `--interface-stats` enables periodic interface counter sampling; leave unset to report only lifecycle and state changes.\n- `--interface-stats-interval` customizes how frequently statistics are collected (for example `500ms`, `2s`, `1m`).\n\nWhen invoked with no arguments it discovers all running labs and immediately begins streaming events; new labs that start after the command begins are picked up automatically.",

        "type": "file",
        "path": "/source/head/docs/cmd/events.md",
        "text": "# events command\n\n## Description\n\nThe `events` command streams lifecycle updates for every Containerlab resource and augments them with interface change notifications collected from the container network namespaces. The output combines the selected runtime's event feed (for example Docker) with the netlink information that powers `containerlab inspect interfaces`, so you can observe container activity and interface state changes in real time without selecting a specific lab.\n\n## Usage\n\n`containerlab [global-flags] events [local-flags]`\n\n**aliases:** `ev`\n\nThe command respects the global flags such as `--runtime`, `--debug`, or `--log-level`. It adds local options:\n\n- `--format` controls the output representation (`plain`, `json`).\n- `--initial-state` emits a snapshot of currently running containers and their interface states before following live updates.\n- `--interface-stats` enables periodic interface counter sampling; leave unset to report only lifecycle and state changes.\n- `--interface-stats-interval` customizes how frequently statistics are collected (for example `500ms`, `2s`, `1m`).\n\nWhen invoked with no arguments it discovers all running labs and immediately begins streaming events; new labs that start after the command begins are picked up automatically.\n\n## Event format\n\nIn the default `plain` format every line mirrors the `docker events` format:\n\n```\n<timestamp> <type> <action> <actor> (<key>=<value>, ...)\n```\n\n- **Runtime events** show the short container ID as the actor and include the original attributes supplied by the container runtime (for example `image`, `name`, `containerlab`, `scope`, …). Container events are also enriched with `mgmt_ipv4`, `mgmt_ipv6`, and `ports` (published/exposed ports) when those values are available. When `--initial-state` is enabled the stream starts with `container <state>` snapshots (for example `container running`) that carry an `origin=snapshot` attribute.\n- **Interface events** use type `interface` and `origin=netlink` in the attribute list. They also report interface-specific data such as `ifname`, `state`, `mtu`, `mac`, `type`, `alias`, and the lab label. The actor is still the container short ID, and the container name is supplied in the attributes (`name=...`).\n- Interface notifications are emitted when a link appears, disappears, or when its relevant properties (operational state, MTU, alias, MAC address, type) change. Initial snapshots use the `snapshot` action when `--initial-state` is requested. When interface statistics are enabled the stream also includes `interface stats` updates with byte/packet counters and rate estimates.\n\nWhen `--format json` is used, each event becomes a single JSON object on its own line. The fields match the plain output (`timestamp`, `type`, `action`, `actor_id`, `actor_name`, `actor_full_id`) and include an `attributes` map with the same key/value pairs that the plain formatter prints.\n\n## Examples\n\n### Watch an existing lab and new deployments\n\n```\n$ containerlab events\n2024-07-01T11:02:56.123456000Z container start 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (containerlab=frr-lab, image=ghcr.io/srl-labs/frr, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, ports=0.0.0.0:2222/tcp->22;0.0.0.0:830/tcp->830)\n2024-07-01T11:02:57.004321000Z interface create 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (ifname=eth0, index=22, lab=frr-lab, mac=02:42:ac:14:00:02, mtu=1500, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=netlink, state=up, type=veth)\n2024-07-01T11:02:57.104512000Z interface update 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (ifname=eth0, index=22, lab=frr-lab, mac=02:42:ac:14:00:02, mtu=9000, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=netlink, state=up, type=veth)\n2024-07-01T11:05:12.918273000Z container die 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (containerlab=frr-lab, exitCode=0, image=ghcr.io/srl-labs/frr, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01)\n2024-07-01T11:05:13.018456000Z interface delete 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (ifname=eth0, index=22, lab=frr-lab, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=netlink, state=up, type=veth)\n```\n\nThe stream contains all currently running labs and stays active to capture subsequent deployments, restarts, or interface adjustments.\n\n### Include existing resources in the stream\n\n```\n$ containerlab events --initial-state\n2024-07-01T11:02:55.912345000Z container running 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (containerlab=frr-lab, image=ghcr.io/srl-labs/frr, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=snapshot, ports=0.0.0.0:2222/tcp->22;0.0.0.0:830/tcp->830, state=running)\n2024-07-01T11:02:55.912678000Z interface snapshot 5d0b5a9ad3f1 (ifname=eth0, index=22, lab=frr-lab, mac=02:42:ac:14:00:02, mtu=1500, name=clab-frr-lab-frr01, origin=netlink, state=up, type=veth)\n…\n```\n\nThis mode begins with a point-in-time view of every running container and interface before switching to live updates.\n\n### Include interface statistics\n\n```\ncontainerlab events --interface-stats\n```\n\nStatistics are disabled by default. Enabling them augments the feed with periodic counter samples in addition to lifecycle and state changes. Use `--interface-stats-interval` to balance fidelity with overhead: values between `1s` and `5s` work well for most labs, while larger deployments may prefer longer intervals (for example `10s`) to avoid excessive sampling load.\n\n### Use with alternative runtimes\n\nContainerlab streams events from the runtime selected via the global `--runtime` flag.\n\nCurrently supported runtimes:\n\n- `docker`\n- `c9s`\n\nWith `--runtime c9s`, events are backed by kubernetes watches for Clabernetes\n`Topology` and `Node` resources and labeled Pods. They use the actions and\nattributes of the Docker runtime: a node is a container (`create`, `start`,\n`kill`, `stop`, `die`, `destroy`, `health_status: …`) described by the same\nlabels, and the interface events (`snapshot`, `create`, `update`, `delete`,\n`stats`) report the node's lab link interfaces, `eth0` and `lo` under the names\na Docker container of the node has. Interface state and statistics are sampled\ninside the device containers, not through local host netlink. The stream covers\nevery namespace by default; `--namespace` (or `CLAB_KUBE_NAMESPACE`) limits it\nto one namespace, and `-t`/`--name` to one lab. With Docker and Podman the lab\nselection is ignored and every lab is streamed. The\n[c9s runtime manual](../manual/clabernetes/runtime.md#events) lists the\nattributes and the few Docker attributes c9s has no equivalent for.\n\nRuntimes that do not implement the `events` API, or are not yet supported by Containerlab, will exit with an explanatory error.\n\n## See also\n\n- [`inspect interfaces`](inspect/interfaces.md) – produces a point-in-time view of the same interface details that `events` reports continuously.\n- [Clabernetes containerlab runtime](../manual/clabernetes/runtime.md#events) - describes c9s events and how they map onto Docker events.\n- `docker events` – the raw runtime feed that Containerlab builds upon.",
